    selectable.Item = self:Add(selectable.Parts.Button, { Height = Util.Metric("Item"), Search = Row.SearchText(info) })
    selectable.Parts.SetSelected(selectable.Value, true)
    selectable.Parts.Button.Activated:Connect(function()
        selectable:SetValue(not selectable.Value)
    end)
    Widget.Register(selectable, idx, info)
    return selectable
end

function Selectable:SetValue(value)
    value = value == true
    if value == self.Value then
        return
    end
    self.Value = value
    self.Parts.SetSelected(value)
    self:Fire()
end

function Container:AddListBox(idx, info)
    idx, info = Widget.Normalize(idx, info)
    local rows = info.Height or 5
    local itemHeight = Util.Metric("Item")
    local listbox = setmetatable({ Type = "Dropdown", Multi = info.Multi == true, AllowNull = info.AllowNull ~= false, Values = info.Values or {}, Container = self, Entries = {} }, ListBox)
    listbox.Value = listbox.Multi and Util.ToSet(info.Default) or info.Default
    listbox.Row = Row.Stack(self, info, rows * (itemHeight + 2) + 10)
    listbox.Item = listbox.Row.Item
    local frame = Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = listbox.Row.Control }, "Element", "Outline", 10, 2)
    local scroll = Layout.ScrollFrame({ Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = frame })
    listbox.List = Container.New(scroll, { PadX = 4, PadY = 4, GapY = 2 })
    listbox.List:SetParent(self)
    listbox.Row.OnArranged = function(width)
        listbox.List:SetWidth(width - Config.Page.ScrollBar - 4)
    end
    Widget.Register(listbox, idx, info)
    listbox:Rebuild()
    return listbox
end

function ListBox:Rebuild()
    self.List:Clear()
    table.clear(self.Entries)
    for _, value in ipairs(self.Values) do
        local parts = Selectable.Build(value, { Multi = self.Multi })
        parts.SetSelected(Dropdown.IsSelected(self, value), true)
        parts.Button.Activated:Connect(function()
            Dropdown.Pick(self, value)
        end)
        self.Entries[value] = parts
        self.List:Add(parts.Button, { Height = Util.Metric("Item") })
    end
    if #self.Values == 0 then
        self.List:AddLabel(Lang.Strings.Empty)
    end
end

function ListBox:Render()
    for value, parts in pairs(self.Entries) do
        parts.SetSelected(Dropdown.IsSelected(self, value))
    end
end

ListBox.IsSelected = Dropdown.IsSelected
ListBox.GetActiveValues = Dropdown.GetActiveValues
ListBox.Serialize = Dropdown.Serialize

function ListBox:SetValue(value)
    self.Value = self.Multi and Util.ToSet(value) or value
    self:Render()
    self:Fire()
end

function ListBox:SetValues(values)
    self.Values = values or {}
    if not self.Multi and self.Value ~= nil and not table.find(self.Values, self.Value) then
        self.Value = nil
    end
    self:Rebuild()
end

function Container:AddProgressBar(idx, info)
    idx, info = Widget.Normalize(idx, info)
    local progress = setmetatable({ Type = "Progress", Value = 0, Max = info.Max or 1, Suffix = info.Suffix, Container = self }, Progress)
    progress.Row = Row.Stack(self, info, 16)
    progress.Item = progress.Row.Item
    local bar = Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = progress.Row.Control }, "Track", "Outline", UDim.new(1, 0), 2)
    progress.Fill = Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Parent = bar }, "Good", nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local shine = Draw.Box("Frame", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 2), Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 3), BackgroundTransparency = 0.55, Parent = progress.Fill }, "White", nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
    shine.ZIndex = 2
    progress:SetValue(info.Default or info.Value or 0)
    if idx ~= nil then
        Library.Options[idx] = progress
        progress.NoSave = true
    end
    return progress
end

function Progress:SetValue(value)
    self.Value = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 0, 0, self.Max)
    local fraction = self.Max > 0 and self.Value / self.Max or 0
    Anim.Tween(self.Fill, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(fraction, 1) }, Config.Tween.Slide)
    local text = self.Suffix and (tostring(Util.Round(self.Value, 2)) .. self.Suffix) or (math.floor(fraction * 100 + 0.5) .. "%")
    local resized = #text ~= #self.Row.Aside.Text
    self.Row.Aside.Text = text
    if resized then
        self.Container:MarkDirty()
    end
end

function Progress:SetText(text)
    self.Row:SetTitle(text)
end

function Progress:SetMax(max)
    self.Max = math.max(1e-6, tonumber(max) or 1)
    self:SetValue(self.Value)
end

function Progress:SetProgress(fraction)
    self:SetValue((tonumber(fraction) or 0) * self.Max)
end

Progress.SetVisible = Widget.SetVisible

--@param info { Height = px (nil = auto-fit content), Border = true }
function Container:AddChild(id, info)
    if Gui and Gui.BeginChild then
        return Gui.BeginChild(self, id, info)
    end
    if type(id) == "table" and info == nil then
        info, id = id, nil
    end
    info = info or {}
    local holder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = tostring(id or "Child"), BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
    local frame = Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = holder }, info.Border ~= false and "Element" or nil, info.Border ~= false and "Track" or nil, 10, 2)
    if info.Border ~= false then
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
    end
    local host = info.Height and Layout.ScrollFrame({ Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = frame }) or Draw.New("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = frame })
    local child = Container.New(host, { PadX = 8, PadY = 8 })
    local inset = info.Height and Config.Page.ScrollBar or 0
    self:Add(holder, {
        Height = function()
            return info.Height or child.ContentHeight
        end,
        Child = child,
        ChildInset = inset,
    })
    return child
end

Container.BeginChild = Container.AddChild

function Container:AddKeybind(idx, info)
    idx, info = Widget.Normalize(idx, info)
    local row = Row.New(self, info)
    local picker = KeyPicker.New(row, idx, info, nil)
    picker.Row, picker.Item, picker.Container = row, row.Item, self
    return picker
end

function Container:AddColorPicker(idx, info)
    idx, info = Widget.Normalize(idx, info)
    local row = Row.New(self, info)
    local picker = ColorPicker.New(row, idx, info)
    picker.Row, picker.Item, picker.Container = row, row.Item, self
    return picker
end

-- Extended Framework Widgets (WindUI + SpellUI + ReactBits)
function Container:AddStepper(idx, spec)
    idx, spec = Widget.Normalize(idx, spec)
    local stepVal = spec.Step or 1
    local stepper = setmetatable({
        Type = "Stepper",
        Min = spec.Min or -1e9,
        Max = spec.Max or 1e9,
        Step = stepVal,
        Rounding = spec.Rounding or (math.floor(stepVal) ~= stepVal and 2 or 0),
        Container = self,
    }, Stepper)
    stepper.Value = Util.Round(math.clamp(tonumber(spec.Default) or math.max(0, stepper.Min), stepper.Min, stepper.Max), stepper.Rounding)
    stepper.Row = Row.New(self, spec)
    stepper.Item = stepper.Row.Item
    stepper:Build()
    Widget.Register(stepper, idx, spec)
    stepper:Render(true)
    return stepper
end

function Stepper:Build()
    local height = Util.Metric("Box") - 4
    local box = Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(108, height) }, "Element", "Outline", 8, 2)
    local minusBtn = Draw.Box("TextButton", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(height - 4, height - 4), Parent = box }, "Hover", nil, 6)
    Icons.Vector(minusBtn, "minus", 14, "Text").Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.18, 0.18)
    local plusBtn = Draw.Box("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(height - 4, height - 4), Parent = box }, "Accent", nil, 6)
    Icons.Vector(plusBtn, "plus", 14, "OnAccent").Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.18, 0.18)
    self.Readout = Draw.Text({
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(height, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -height * 2, 1, 0),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        Parent = box,
    }, "Strong", Util.TextSize("Label"), "Text", tostring(self.Value))
    minusBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
        Fx.Squash(minusBtn, 1.8)
        self:SetValue(self.Value - self.Step)
    end)
    plusBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
        Fx.Squash(plusBtn, 1.8)
        self:SetValue(self.Value + self.Step)
    end)
    self.Row:AddRight(box, 108)
end

function Stepper:Render(instant)
    local formatted = self.Rounding > 0 and string.format("%." .. self.Rounding .. "f", self.Value) or tostring(math.floor(self.Value + 0.5))
    if instant or not self.PrevValue then
        self.Readout.Text = formatted
    else
        Fx.CountUp(self, self.PrevValue, self.Value, 0.18, function(cur)
            local num = Util.Round(cur, self.Rounding)
            self.Readout.Text = self.Rounding > 0 and string.format("%." .. self.Rounding .. "f", num) or tostring(math.floor(num + 0.5))
        end)
    end
    self.PrevValue = self.Value
end

function Stepper:SetValue(nextVal)
    local num = tonumber(nextVal)
    if not num then
        return
    end
    num = Util.Round(math.clamp(num, self.Min, self.Max), self.Rounding)
    if num == self.Value then
        return
    end
    self.Value = num
    self:Render(false)
    self:Fire()
end

function Container:AddRangeSlider(idx, spec)
    idx, spec = Widget.Normalize(idx, spec)
    local knobSize = Util.Metric("Knob")
    local minBound, maxBound = spec.Min or 0, spec.Max or 100
    local defTable = type(spec.Default) == "table" and spec.Default or { minBound, maxBound }
    local range = setmetatable({
        Type = "RangeSlider",
        Min = minBound,
        Max = maxBound,
        Rounding = spec.Rounding or 0,
        Suffix = spec.Suffix or "",
        Container = self,
    }, RangeSlider)
    local low = Util.Round(math.clamp(tonumber(defTable[1]) or minBound, minBound, maxBound), range.Rounding)
    local high = Util.Round(math.clamp(tonumber(defTable[2]) or maxBound, low, maxBound), range.Rounding)
    range.Value = { low, high }
    range.Row = Row.Stack(self, spec, knobSize + 4)
    range.Item = range.Row.Item
    range:Build(knobSize)
    Widget.Register(range, idx, spec)
    range:Render(false)
    return range
end

function RangeSlider:Build(knobSize)
    local control = self.Row.Control
    local trackHeight = Util.Metric("Track")
    self.Track = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, knobSize / 2, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -knobSize, 0, trackHeight),
        Parent = control,
    }, "Track", "Outline", UDim.new(1, 0), 2)
    self.Fill = Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = self.Track }, "Accent", nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
    self.KnobLow = Draw.Box("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(knobSize, knobSize), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5), ZIndex = 3, Parent = self.Track }, "Coin", "Outline", UDim.new(1, 0), 2)
    self.KnobHigh = Draw.Box("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(knobSize, knobSize), Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.5), ZIndex = 4, Parent = self.Track }, "Coin", "Outline", UDim.new(1, 0), 2)
    local hit = Draw.New("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 6), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, -3), ZIndex = 5, Parent = control })
    hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if Util.IsPointer(input) then
            self:BeginDrag(input)
        end
    end)
end

function RangeSlider:BeginDrag(input)
    local left, width = self.Track.AbsolutePosition.X, math.max(1, self.Track.AbsoluteSize.X)
    local frac = math.clamp((input.Position.X - left) / width, 0, 1)
    local pickedVal = self.Min + (self.Max - self.Min) * frac
    local pickHigh = math.abs(pickedVal - self.Value[2]) < math.abs(pickedVal - self.Value[1])
    local activeKnob = pickHigh and self.KnobHigh or self.KnobLow
    Fx.Squash(activeKnob, 1.9)
    local function UpdateFromX(screenX)
        local ratio = math.clamp((screenX - self.Track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, self.Track.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
        local raw = self.Min + (self.Max - self.Min) * ratio
        if pickHigh then
            self:SetValue({ self.Value[1], math.max(self.Value[1], raw) })
        else
            self:SetValue({ math.min(self.Value[2], raw), self.Value[2] })
        end
    end
    UpdateFromX(input.Position.X)
    State.Drag = {
        Move = function(move)
            UpdateFromX(move.Position.X)
        end,
        Stop = function()
            Fx.Squash(activeKnob, 1.4)
        end,
    }
end

function RangeSlider:Render(animated)
    local span = math.max(1e-6, self.Max - self.Min)
    local lowFrac = math.clamp((self.Value[1] - self.Min) / span, 0, 1)
    local highFrac = math.clamp((self.Value[2] - self.Min) / span, lowFrac, 1)
    if animated then
        Motion.Spring(self.Fill, "Position", UDim2.fromScale(lowFrac, 0), "Fast")
        Motion.Spring(self.Fill, "Size", UDim2.fromScale(highFrac - lowFrac, 1), "Fast")
        Motion.Spring(self.KnobLow, "Position", UDim2.fromScale(lowFrac, 0.5), "Fast")
        Motion.Spring(self.KnobHigh, "Position", UDim2.fromScale(highFrac, 0.5), "Fast")
    else
        Motion.Set(self.Fill, "Position", UDim2.fromScale(lowFrac, 0))
        Motion.Set(self.Fill, "Size", UDim2.fromScale(highFrac - lowFrac, 1))
        Motion.Set(self.KnobLow, "Position", UDim2.fromScale(lowFrac, 0.5))
        Motion.Set(self.KnobHigh, "Position", UDim2.fromScale(highFrac, 0.5))
    end
    local suf = Lang.Resolve(self.Suffix or "")
    self.Row.Aside.Text = tostring(self.Value[1]) .. suf .. " – " .. tostring(self.Value[2]) .. suf
end

function RangeSlider:SetValue(pair)
    if type(pair) ~= "table" then
        return
    end
    local low = Util.Round(math.clamp(tonumber(pair[1]) or self.Min, self.Min, self.Max), self.Rounding)
    local high = Util.Round(math.clamp(tonumber(pair[2]) or self.Max, low, self.Max), self.Rounding)
    if low == self.Value[1] and high == self.Value[2] then
        return
    end
    self.Value = { low, high }
    self:Render(true)
    self:Fire()
end

function Container:AddSegmented(idx, spec)
    idx, spec = Widget.Normalize(idx, spec)
    local optionsList = spec.Values or spec.Options or {}
    local seg = setmetatable({
        Type = "Segmented",
        Values = optionsList,
        Value = spec.Default or optionsList[1],
        Buttons = {},
        Container = self,
    }, Segmented)
    seg.Row = (spec.Text or spec.Title) and Row.Stack(self, spec, Util.Metric("Box")) or nil
    local host = seg.Row and seg.Row.Control or Draw.New("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
    if not seg.Row then
        seg.Item = self:Add(host, { Height = Util.Metric("Box"), Search = Lang.SearchText(spec.Text) })
    else
        seg.Item = seg.Row.Item
    end
    seg:Build(host)
    Widget.Register(seg, idx, spec)
    seg:Render(true)
    return seg
end

function Segmented:Build(host)
    local bar = Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = host }, "Element", "Outline", 8, 2)
    Draw.Padding(bar, 3, 3, 3, 3)
    local count = math.max(1, #self.Values)
    self.Thumb = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1 / count, 1),
        ZIndex = 2,
        Parent = bar,
    }, "Accent", nil, 6)
    for index, optionVal in ipairs(self.Values) do
        local btn = Draw.Text({
            ClassName = "TextButton",
            Position = UDim2.fromScale((index - 1) / count, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1 / count, 1),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            ZIndex = 3,
            Parent = bar,
        }, "Strong", Util.TextSize("Label") - 1, "Text")
        Lang.Bind(btn, type(optionVal) == "table" and optionVal or Dropdown.FormatValue(optionVal))
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            self:SetValue(optionVal)
        end)
        self.Buttons[index] = { Button = btn, Value = optionVal }
    end
end

function Segmented:Render(instant)
    local count = math.max(1, #self.Values)
    local activeIdx = 1
    for index, entry in ipairs(self.Buttons) do
        local isSel = entry.Value == self.Value
        if isSel then
            activeIdx = index
        end
        Theme.Bind(entry.Button, { TextColor3 = isSel and "OnAccent" or "SubText" })
    end
    local targetPos = UDim2.fromScale((activeIdx - 1) / count, 0)
    local targetSize = UDim2.fromScale(1 / count, 1)
    if instant then
        Motion.Set(self.Thumb, "Position", targetPos)
        Motion.Set(self.Thumb, "Size", targetSize)
    else
        Motion.Spring(self.Thumb, "Position", targetPos, "Fast", { Damping = 0.68 })
        Motion.Spring(self.Thumb, "Size", targetSize, "Fast")
    end
end

function Segmented:SetValue(nextVal)
    if nextVal == self.Value then
        return
    end
    self.Value = nextVal
    self:Render(false)
    self:Fire()
end

function Container:AddStat(idx, spec)
    idx, spec = Widget.Normalize(idx, spec)
    local card = Draw.Box("Frame", { Name = "StatCard" }, "Element", "Outline", 10, 2)
    Fx.Spotlight(card)
    local badge = Icons.Badge(card, spec.Icon or "stat", 30, spec.Color or "Accent", spec.Title or spec.Text)
    badge.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    badge.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0)
    local titleLabel = Draw.Text({
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 6),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 16),
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        Parent = card,
    }, "Desc", Util.TextSize("Desc"), "SubText", spec.Title or spec.Text or "Stat")
    local valLabel = Draw.Text({
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 22),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 22),
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        Parent = card,
    }, "Display", Util.TextSize("Group") + 2, spec.Color or "Text", tostring(spec.Value or spec.Default or "0"))
    local stat = setmetatable({
        Type = "Stat",
        Frame = card,
        TitleLabel = titleLabel,
        ValueLabel = valLabel,
        Prefix = spec.Prefix or "",
        Suffix = spec.Suffix or "",
        Value = spec.Value or spec.Default or 0,
        Container = self,
    }, StatWidget)
    stat.Item = self:Add(card, { Width = spec.Width, Height = 50, Search = Row.SearchText(spec) })
    Widget.Register(stat, idx, spec)
    return stat
end

function StatWidget:SetValue(nextVal)
    local prevNum = tonumber(self.Value)
    local nextNum = tonumber(nextVal)
    self.Value = nextVal
    local pre = Lang.Resolve(self.Prefix or "")
    local suf = Lang.Resolve(self.Suffix or "")
    if prevNum and nextNum then
        Fx.CountUp(self, prevNum, nextNum, 0.25, function(cur)
            local formatted
            if cur == math.floor(cur) then
                formatted = tostring(math.floor(cur))
            else
                formatted = string.format("%.2f", cur):gsub("%.?0+$", "")
            end
            self.ValueLabel.Text = pre .. formatted .. suf
        end)
    else
        self.ValueLabel.Text = pre .. tostring(nextVal) .. suf
    end
end

function Container:AddStatus(idx, spec)
    idx, spec = Widget.Normalize(idx, spec)
    local row = Row.New(self, spec)
    local pill, label = Gui.Badge(row.Right, spec.Status or spec.Default or "Ready", spec.Color or "Good", true)
    row:AddRight(pill.Frame, pill.Width)
    local status = setmetatable({
        Type = "Status",
        Row = row,
        Item = row.Item,
        Badge = pill,
        Label = label,
        Value = spec.Status or spec.Default or "Ready",
        Container = self,
    }, StatusWidget)
    Widget.Register(status, idx, spec)
    return status
end

function StatusWidget:SetValue(nextText, colorToken)
    self.Value = nextText
    self.Badge:SetText(nextText)
    if colorToken then
        self.Badge:SetColor(colorToken)
    end
end

function Container:AddTable(idx, spec)
    idx, spec = Widget.Normalize(idx, spec)
    local headers = spec.Headers or spec.Columns or {}
    local rows = spec.Rows or {}
    local holder = Draw.Box("Frame", { Name = "Table" }, "Element", "Outline", 10, 2)
    local inner = Container.New(holder, { PadX = 8, PadY = 6, GapY = 4 })
    inner:SetParent(self)
    local tbl = setmetatable({
        Type = "Table",
        Headers = headers,
        Rows = rows,
        Inner = inner,
        Container = self,
    }, TableWidget)
    tbl.Item = self:Add(holder, {
        Height = function()
            return math.max(36, inner.ContentHeight)
        end,
        Child = inner,
        Search = Row.SearchText(spec),
    })
    tbl:Rebuild()
    return tbl
end

function TableWidget:Rebuild()
    self.Inner:Clear()
    local colCount = math.max(1, #self.Headers)
    if #self.Headers > 0 then
        local headRow = Draw.Box("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 }, "PanelHeader", nil, 6)
        for colIdx, hText in ipairs(self.Headers) do
            Draw.Text({
                Position = UDim2.new((colIdx - 1) / colCount, 6, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1 / colCount, -8, 1, 0),
                TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                Parent = headRow,
            }, "Strong", Util.TextSize("Desc"), "Accent", hText)
        end
        self.Inner:Add(headRow, { Height = 24 })
    end
    for _, cells in ipairs(self.Rows) do
        local line = Draw.New("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
        for colIdx = 1, colCount do
            Draw.Text({
                Position = UDim2.new((colIdx - 1) / colCount, 6, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1 / colCount, -8, 1, 0),
                TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                Parent = line,
            }, "Body", Util.TextSize("Desc"), colIdx == 1 and "Text" or "SubText", tostring(cells[colIdx] or ""))
        end
        self.Inner:Add(line, { Height = 22 })
    end
    self.Container:MarkDirty()
end

function TableWidget:SetRows(nextRows)
    self.Rows = nextRows or {}
    self:Rebuild()
end

function TableWidget:AddRow(row)
    table.insert(self.Rows, row)
    self:Rebuild()
end

function TableWidget:Clear()
    self.Rows = {}
    self:Rebuild()
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  NEW 8-BIT FUNCTIONAL OVERVIEW WIDGETS
--  1. AddMonitor: 2D Tactical Canvas Radar / Map / Entity Tracker
--  2. AddStatGrid: Multi-Metric Telemetry Cards Grid
--  3. AddLiveGraph: 8-Bit Scrolling Performance Stream Strip-Chart
--  4. AddInventoryGrid: Stardew Valley / RPG Slot Matrix
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local Monitor = {}
Monitor.__index = Monitor

function Container:AddMonitor(idx, info)
    idx, info = Widget.Normalize(idx, info)
    info.Height = info.Height or 230
    info.Range = info.Range or 250
    local monitor = setmetatable({
        Type = "Monitor",
        Container = self,
        Info = info,
        Range = info.Range,
        Entities = {},
        ActiveFilters = {},
        BlipFrames = {},
    }, Monitor)

    if info.Filters then
        for _, f in ipairs(info.Filters) do
            monitor.ActiveFilters[f] = true
        end
    end

    local holder = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "MonitorHolder",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, info.Height),
    })

    local shadow = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(Config.Group.Shadow, Config.Group.Shadow),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -Config.Group.Shadow, 1, -Config.Group.Shadow),
        Parent = holder,
    }, "Shadow", nil, "Group")

    local card = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -Config.Group.Shadow, 1, -Config.Group.Shadow),
        Parent = holder,
    }, "Panel", "Outline", "Group", Config.Group.Stroke)

    -- Header bar with Title, Range badge, and Controls
    local header = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Name = "MonitorHeader",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28),
        Parent = card,
    }, "PanelHeader", nil, "Group")

    local titleLabel = Draw.Text({
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 1, 0),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = header,
    }, "Body", Util.TextSize("Label"), "Text", info.Title or info.Text or "2D Tactical Radar")

    local headerRight = Draw.New("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -4),
        Parent = header,
    })
    Draw.List(headerRight, 4, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)

    -- Zoom buttons
    local zoomOutBtn = Draw.Box("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20),
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        LayoutOrder = 10,
        Parent = headerRight,
    }, "Element", "Outline", 4, 1)
    Draw.Text({ Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = zoomOutBtn }, "Strong", 13, "Text", "-")

    local zoomInBtn = Draw.Box("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20),
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        LayoutOrder = 11,
        Parent = headerRight,
    }, "Element", "Outline", 4, 1)
    Draw.Text({ Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = zoomInBtn }, "Strong", 13, "Text", "+")

    -- Range Badge
    local rangeBadge = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(68, 20),
        LayoutOrder = 9,
        Parent = headerRight,
    }, "Element", "Outline", 4, 1)
    local rangeText = Draw.Text({
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        Parent = rangeBadge,
    }, "Desc", 10, "Coin", tostring(monitor.Range) .. "m")

    -- Radar Screen Canvas
    local canvas = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "RadarCanvas",
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 28),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -28),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = card,
    })

    -- Screen background with dark CRT phosphor tint
    local screenBg = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        Parent = canvas,
    }, "BackdropAlt", nil, 0)
    screenBg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2

    -- Concentric Range Rings (50m, 100m, 200m)
    local ringsHolder = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "Rings",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        Parent = canvas,
    })
    for _, frac in ipairs({ 0.28, 0.56, 0.84 }) do
        local ring = Draw.New("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(frac, frac),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Parent = ringsHolder,
        })
        Draw.Corner(ring, UDim.new(1, 0))
        local stroke = Draw.New("UIStroke", {
            Thickness = 1,
            Transparency = 0.75,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
            Parent = ring,
        })
        Theme.Bind(stroke, { Color = "Outline" })
    end

    -- Crosshair Axis lines
    local hLine = Draw.New("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.75,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = canvas,
    })
    Theme.Bind(hLine, { BackgroundColor3 = "Outline" })

    local vLine = Draw.New("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.75,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = canvas,
    })
    Theme.Bind(vLine, { BackgroundColor3 = "Outline" })

    -- Center Player Icon (You / Crosshair origin)
    local centerDot = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "PlayerCenter",
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8),
        ZIndex = 6,
        Parent = canvas,
    })
    Draw.Corner(centerDot, UDim.new(1, 0))
    Theme.Bind(centerDot, { BackgroundColor3 = "Accent" })
    local pStroke = Draw.Stroke(centerDot, "White", 1.5, true)
    pStroke.Transparency = 0.2

    -- Facing Arrow on player
    local headingArrow = Draw.New("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 6),
        ZIndex = 7,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = centerDot,
    })
    Theme.Bind(headingArrow, { BackgroundColor3 = "Accent" })

    -- Blip layer
    local blipLayer = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "BlipLayer",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ZIndex = 5,
        Parent = canvas,
    })

    monitor.Holder = holder
    monitor.Card = card
    monitor.Canvas = canvas
    monitor.BlipLayer = blipLayer
    monitor.RangeText = rangeText
    monitor.HeadingArrow = headingArrow
    monitor.CenterDot = centerDot

    -- Zoom handlers
    zoomInBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
        monitor:SetRange(math.max(50, monitor.Range - 50))
    end)
    zoomOutBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
        monitor:SetRange(math.min(1000, monitor.Range + 50))
    end)

    monitor.Item = self:Add(holder, { Height = info.Height })
    Widget.Register(monitor, idx, info)
    return monitor
end

function Monitor:SetRange(range)
    self.Range = range
    if self.RangeText then
        self.RangeText.Text = tostring(range) .. "m"
    end
    self:RenderAllBlips()
end

function Monitor:GetRange()
    return self.Range
end

function Monitor:AddEntity(id, spec)
    self.Entities[id] = {
        Id = id,
        Name = spec.Name or tostring(id),
        Position = spec.Position or Vector3.new(),
        Type = spec.Type or "Default",
        Color = spec.Color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60),
        Size = spec.Size or 6,
    }
    self:RenderBlip(id)
end

function Monitor:UpdateEntity(id, spec)
    local ent = self.Entities[id]
    if not ent then
        self:AddEntity(id, spec)
        return
    end
    if spec.Position then ent.Position = spec.Position end
    if spec.Color then ent.Color = spec.Color end
    if spec.Name then ent.Name = spec.Name end
    if spec.Type then ent.Type = spec.Type end
    self:RenderBlip(id)
end

function Monitor:RemoveEntity(id)
    self.Entities[id] = nil
    if self.BlipFrames[id] then
        self.BlipFrames[id]:Destroy()
        self.BlipFrames[id] = nil
    end
end

function Monitor:Clear()
    self.Entities = {}
    for id, frame in pairs(self.BlipFrames) do
        frame:Destroy()
    end
    self.BlipFrames = {}
end

function Monitor:RenderBlip(id)
    local ent = self.Entities[id]
    if not ent then return end

    local lp = LocalPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
    local myChar = lp and lp.Character
    local myRoot = myChar and (myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or myChar:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    local myPos = myRoot and myRoot.Position or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    local myCFrame = myRoot and myRoot.CFrame or CFrame.new()

    local entPos = ent.Position
    if typeof(entPos) == "CFrame" then entPos = entPos.Position end

    -- Relative coordinates in camera / player space
    local rel = myCFrame:PointToObjectSpace(entPos)
    local dx = rel.X
    local dz = rel.Z

    local maxRange = self.Range or 250
    local normX = math.clamp(dx / (maxRange * 2), -0.46, 0.46)
    local normY = math.clamp(dz / (maxRange * 2), -0.46, 0.46)

    local frame = self.BlipFrames[id]
    if not frame then
        frame = Draw.New("Frame", {
            Name = "Blip_" .. tostring(id),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ent.Size or 6, ent.Size or 6),
            BackgroundColor3 = ent.Color,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ZIndex = 5,
            Parent = self.BlipLayer,
        })
        Draw.Corner(frame, UDim.new(1, 0))
        self.BlipFrames[id] = frame
    else
        frame.BackgroundColor3 = ent.Color
    end

    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5 + normX, 0, 0.5 + normY, 0)
end

function Monitor:RenderAllBlips()
    for id in pairs(self.Entities) do
        self:RenderBlip(id)
    end
end

function Monitor:TrackWorkspace(options)
    options = options or {}
    local folder = options.Folder or workspace
    local typeFilter = options.Type or "Enemy"
    local color = options.Color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
    local getName = options.GetName or function(inst) return inst.Name end

    local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not self.Holder or not self.Holder.Parent then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            local root = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or child:FindFirstChild("Torso") or (child:IsA("BasePart") and child)
            if root then
                self:UpdateEntity(child, {
                    Name = getName(child),
                    Position = root.Position,
                    Type = typeFilter,
                    Color = color,
                })
            end
        end
    end)
    table.insert(State.Connections, conn)
    return conn
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  2. AddStatGrid: Multi-Metric Telemetry Cards Grid
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local StatGrid = {}
StatGrid.__index = StatGrid

function Container:AddStatGrid(idx, info)
    idx, info = Widget.Normalize(idx, info)
    local cols = info.Columns or 4
    local stats = info.Stats or {}
    local height = info.Height or 68

    local statGrid = setmetatable({
        Type = "StatGrid",
        Container = self,
        Cards = {},
    }, StatGrid)

    local holder = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "StatGridHolder",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
    })

    local grid = Draw.New("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        Parent = holder,
    })
    Draw.List(grid, 6, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)

    for i, sSpec in ipairs(stats) do
        local key = sSpec.Key or sSpec.Id or ("Stat_" .. tostring(i))
        local cardW = math.floor((1 / cols) * 100) / 100

        local card = Draw.Box("Frame", {
            Name = "StatCard_" .. key,
            Size = UDim2.new(cardW, -5, 1, 0),
            Parent = grid,
        }, "Panel", "Outline", 6, 1.5)

        local inner = Draw.New("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 6),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -12),
            Parent = card,
        })
        Draw.List(inner, 2, false, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)

        -- Top row: Icon + Title
        local topRow = Draw.New("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
            Parent = inner,
        })
        Draw.List(topRow, 4, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)

        if sSpec.Icon then
            local icHolder = Draw.New("Frame", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
                Parent = topRow,
            })
            local ic = Icons.Create(icHolder, sSpec.Icon, 14, nil, sSpec.Title)
            if ic then
                ic.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                ic.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            end
        end

        local title = Draw.Text({
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0),
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            Parent = topRow,
        }, "Desc", 10, "SubText", sSpec.Title or key)

        -- Middle row: Big Value
        local valLabel = Draw.Text({
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            Parent = inner,
        }, "Display", 16, sSpec.Color or "Accent", tostring(sSpec.Value or "0"))

        -- Bottom row: SubText / Delta
        local subLabel = Draw.Text({
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14),
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            Parent = inner,
        }, "Desc", 9, "Muted", tostring(sSpec.SubText or ""))

        statGrid.Cards[key] = {
            Card = card,
            ValLabel = valLabel,
            SubLabel = subLabel,
        }
    end

    statGrid.Holder = holder
    statGrid.Item = self:Add(holder, { Height = height })
    Widget.Register(statGrid, idx, info)
    return statGrid
end

function StatGrid:SetStat(key, val, sub, col)
    local card = self.Cards[key]
    if not card then return end
    if val ~= nil then
        card.ValLabel.Text = tostring(val)
    end
    if sub ~= nil then
        card.SubLabel.Text = tostring(sub)
    end
    if col ~= nil then
        Theme.Bind(card.ValLabel, { TextColor3 = col })
    end
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  3. AddLiveGraph: 8-Bit Scrolling Performance Stream Strip-Chart
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local LiveGraph = {}
LiveGraph.__index = LiveGraph

function Container:AddLiveGraph(idx, info)
    idx, info = Widget.Normalize(idx, info)
    local height = info.Height or 110
    local maxSamples = info.MaxSamples or 30
    local suffix = info.Suffix or ""

    local graph = setmetatable({
        Type = "LiveGraph",
        Container = self,
        MaxSamples = maxSamples,
        Samples = {},
        Suffix = suffix,
        Color = info.Color or "Accent",
    }, LiveGraph)

    local holder = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "LiveGraphHolder",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
    })

    local shadow = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(Config.Group.Shadow, Config.Group.Shadow),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -Config.Group.Shadow, 1, -Config.Group.Shadow),
        Parent = holder,
    }, "Shadow", nil, "Group")

    local card = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -Config.Group.Shadow, 1, -Config.Group.Shadow),
        Parent = holder,
    }, "Panel", "Outline", "Group", Config.Group.Stroke)

    -- Header bar: Title, Current Value, Min, Max, Avg
    local header = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "GraphHeader",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 4),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 18),
        Parent = card,
    })

    local title = Draw.Text({
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = header,
    }, "Body", Util.TextSize("Label"), "Text", info.Title or info.Text or "Telemetry Stream")

    local statsRow = Draw.New("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
        Parent = header,
    })
    Draw.List(statsRow, 6, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)

    local currLabel = Draw.Text({
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 16),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        LayoutOrder = 1,
        Parent = statsRow,
    }, "Display", 13, graph.Color, "0" .. suffix)

    -- Graph Display Screen
    local screen = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "GraphScreen",
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 24),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -32),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Parent = card,
    })
    Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = screen }, "BackdropAlt", "Outline", 4, 1)

    -- Historical Bar Columns Container
    local barsHolder = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "Bars",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8),
        Parent = screen,
    })
    Draw.List(barsHolder, 2, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom)

    graph.Holder = holder
    graph.BarsHolder = barsHolder
    graph.CurrLabel = currLabel

    graph.Item = self:Add(holder, { Height = height })
    Widget.Register(graph, idx, info)
    return graph
end

function LiveGraph:Push(val)
    val = tonumber(val) or 0
    table.insert(self.Samples, val)
    if #self.Samples > self.MaxSamples then
        table.remove(self.Samples, 1)
    end

    if self.CurrLabel then
        self.CurrLabel.Text = tostring(math.floor(val + 0.5)) .. self.Suffix
    end

    -- Find Min/Max
    local maxVal = 1
    for _, s in ipairs(self.Samples) do
        if s > maxVal then maxVal = s end
    end

    -- Re-render bars
    self.BarsHolder:ClearAllChildren()
    Draw.List(self.BarsHolder, 2, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom)

    local totalBars = #self.Samples
    local barW = math.max(3, math.floor((self.BarsHolder.AbsoluteSize.X - (totalBars * 2)) / totalBars))

    for _, s in ipairs(self.Samples) do
        local heightRatio = math.clamp(s / maxVal, 0.05, 1)
        local bar = Draw.New("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, barW, heightRatio, 0),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = self.BarsHolder,
        })
        Draw.Corner(bar, 2)
        Theme.Bind(bar, { BackgroundColor3 = self.Color })
    end
end

function LiveGraph:Clear()
    self.Samples = {}
    if self.BarsHolder then
        self.BarsHolder:ClearAllChildren()
    end
end
LiveGraph.AddPoint = LiveGraph.Push

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  4. AddInventoryGrid: Stardew Valley / RPG Slot Matrix
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local InventoryGrid = {}
InventoryGrid.__index = InventoryGrid

function Container:AddInventoryGrid(idx, info)
    idx, info = Widget.Normalize(idx, info)
    local rows = info.Rows or 2
    local cols = info.Columns or 6
    local slotSize = info.SlotSize or 38
    local gap = 4
    local height = rows * (slotSize + gap) + 38

    local inv = setmetatable({
        Type = "InventoryGrid",
        Container = self,
        Slots = {},
        Items = info.Items or {},
        Callback = info.Callback,
    }, InventoryGrid)

    local holder = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "InventoryHolder",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
    })

    local shadow = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(Config.Group.Shadow, Config.Group.Shadow),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -Config.Group.Shadow, 1, -Config.Group.Shadow),
        Parent = holder,
    }, "Shadow", nil, "Group")

    local card = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -Config.Group.Shadow, 1, -Config.Group.Shadow),
        Parent = holder,
    }, "Panel", "Outline", "Group", Config.Group.Stroke)

    -- Header bar
    local header = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "InvHeader",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20),
        Parent = card,
    })
    Draw.Text({
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = header,
    }, "Body", Util.TextSize("Label"), "Text", info.Title or info.Text or "Inventory Grid")

    -- Grid frame
    local gridHolder = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "Grid",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 26),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -30),
        Parent = card,
    })

    local totalSlots = rows * cols
    for i = 1, totalSlots do
        local r = math.floor((i - 1) / cols)
        local c = (i - 1) % cols

        local slot = Draw.Box("TextButton", {
            Name = "Slot_" .. tostring(i),
            Text = "",
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c * (slotSize + gap), r * (slotSize + gap)),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(slotSize, slotSize),
            Parent = gridHolder,
        }, "BackdropAlt", "Outline", 4, 1.5)

        local iconSlot = Draw.New("Frame", {
            Name = "IconSlot",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(slotSize - 8, slotSize - 8),
            Parent = slot,
        })

        local countBadge = Draw.Text({
            Name = "Count",
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -1),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 12),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            Visible = false,
            Parent = slot,
        }, "Desc", 9, "White")
        Draw.Stroke(countBadge, "Ink", 1)

        slot.Activated:Connect(function()
            if inv.Callback then
                inv.Callback(i, inv.Items[i])
            end
        end)

        inv.Slots[i] = {
            Slot = slot,
            IconSlot = iconSlot,
            Count = countBadge,
        }

        if inv.Items[i] then
            inv:SetSlot(i, inv.Items[i])
        end
    end

    inv.Holder = holder
    inv.Item = self:Add(holder, { Height = height })
    Widget.Register(inv, idx, info)
    return inv
end

function InventoryGrid:SetSlot(index, itemData)
    local s = self.Slots[index]
    if not s then return end
    self.Items[index] = itemData

    s.IconSlot:ClearAllChildren()
    if not itemData then
        s.Count.Visible = false
        return
    end

    if itemData.Icon then
        local ic = Icons.Create(s.IconSlot, itemData.Icon, s.IconSlot.AbsoluteSize.X or 24, nil, itemData.Name)
        if ic then
            ic.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            ic.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        end
    end

    if itemData.Count and itemData.Count > 1 then
        s.Count.Text = tostring(itemData.Count)
        s.Count.Visible = true
    else
        s.Count.Visible = false
    end
end

function InventoryGrid:ClearSlot(index)
    self:SetSlot(index, nil)
end

function InventoryGrid:Clear()
    for i = 1, #self.Slots do
        self:SetSlot(i, nil)
    end
end

function InventoryGrid:GetSlot(index)
    return self.Items[index]
end
