function Window:BuildDecor(topbar)
    self.Clouds, self.Stars = {}, {}
    for index, spot in ipairs({ { 0.52, 8, 42 }, { 0.68, 22, 30 } }) do
        local cloud = Draw.Cloud(topbar, spot[3])
        cloud.Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2])
        cloud.ZIndex = 2
        Anim.Loop("DecorClouds", cloud, { Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], 14, 0, spot[2] + 2) }, 4 + index, "Sine", true)
        table.insert(self.Clouds, cloud)
    end
    for index, spot in ipairs({ { 0.51, 10, 12 }, { 0.55, 30, 9 }, { 0.60, 14, 14 }, { 0.64, 32, 10 }, { 0.68, 8, 11 }, { 0.72, 26, 8 } }) do
        local star = Draw.Text({ AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2]), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(spot[3] + 4, spot[3] + 4), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex = 2, Parent = topbar }, "Glyph", spot[3], "DecorColor")
        star.TextTransparency = 0.15
        Anim.Loop("DecorStars", star, { TextTransparency = 0.8 }, 0.9 + index * 0.37, "Sine", true)
        table.insert(self.Stars, star)
    end
    self.Spooky = {}
    for index, spot in ipairs({ { 0.5, 10, 30, "pumpkin" }, { 0.55, 26, 20, "bat" }, { 0.6, 6, 30, "boo" }, { 0.645, 28, 18, "candy" }, { 0.69, 10, 30, "pumpkin" }, { 0.735, 20, 20, "bat" } }) do
        local item = Sprite.New(topbar, spot[4], spot[3])
        item.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        item.Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2])
        item.ZIndex = 2
        local swing = spot[4] == "bat" and 14 or 6
        Anim.Loop("DecorSpooky", item, { Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2] + (spot[4] == "bat" and 6 or 3)), Rotation = index % 2 == 0 and swing or -swing }, 1.2 + index * 0.35, "Sine", true)
        table.insert(self.Spooky, item)
    end
    self:BuildSpookyBody()
    self:BuildKirbyDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildPokemonDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildAnimalCrossingDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildStardewDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildTamagotchiDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildCastleDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildUndergroundDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildIceDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildDesertDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildGhostHouseDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildStarRoadDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildJungleDecor(topbar)
    self:BuildSkyWorldDecor(topbar)
    Theme.OnRender(self.Stars, function()
        self:RenderDecor()
    end)
    self:RenderDecor()
end

-- Halloween window accents: orange glow border, purple bottom fog, hanging spider
function Window:BuildSpookyBody()
    self.SpookyBody = {}
    local shadow, radius = Config.Window.Shadow, Config.Window.Radius
    local glow = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "Glow", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-3, -3), Size = UDim2.new(1, 6 - shadow, 1, 6 - shadow), ZIndex = 0, Parent = self.Pop })
    Draw.Corner(glow, radius + 3)
    local glowStroke = Draw.New("UIStroke", { Thickness = 4, Transparency = 0.25, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = glow })
    Theme.OnRender(glowStroke, function()
        glowStroke.Color = Theme.Colors.Glow or Theme.Colors.Accent
    end)
    Anim.Loop("DecorSpookyGlow", glowStroke, { Transparency = 0.75, Thickness = 2 }, 1.8, "Sine", true)
    self.SpookyGlow = glow
    table.insert(self.SpookyBody, glow)

    local fog = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "Fog", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 70), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1, Parent = self.Body })
    Draw.Corner(fog, radius)
    local fogShape = Draw.New("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.72) }), Parent = fog })
    Theme.OnRender(fog, function()
        fog.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Colors.Fog or Theme.Colors.Accent
    end)
    Anim.Loop("DecorSpookyBody", fogShape, { Offset = Vector2.new(0, 0.12) }, 3.2, "Sine", true)
    self.SpookyFog = fog
    table.insert(self.SpookyBody, fog)

    local hanger = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "Spider", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, Config.Window.Topbar + 60), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), ZIndex = 9, Parent = self.Body })
    Draw.New("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 300), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 186, 222), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5, Parent = hanger })
    local spider = Sprite.New(hanger, "spider", 16)
    Anim.Loop("DecorSpookyBody", hanger, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, Config.Window.Topbar + 150) }, 3.4, "Sine", true)
    Anim.Loop("DecorSpookyBody", spider, { Rotation = 10 }, 0.9, "Sine", true)
    self.SpookySpider = hanger
    table.insert(self.SpookyBody, hanger)
end


-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  KIRBY THEME DECORATION — Dream Land Warp Stars + Puffball Corner Pip
--  Floating Warp Star sprites bounce in topbar like Halloween pumpkins.
--  A soft pink cloud-puff strip hugs the window bottom (matching SpookyFog).
--  Kirby himself peeks from the right edge, bobbing on a thread of starlight.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function Window:BuildKirbyDecor(topbar)
    self.KirbyDecor = {}
    self.KirbyBody = {}
    local starSpots = {
        { 0.52, 8,  22 }, { 0.57, 20, 20 }, { 0.62, 6, 24 },
        { 0.67, 22, 18 }, { 0.72, 10, 22 },
    }
    for idx, spot in ipairs(starSpots) do
        local s = Sprite.New(topbar, "kirby_star", 22)
        s.AnchorPoint  = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        s.Position     = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2])
        s.ZIndex       = 2
        local rise = idx % 2 == 0 and 6 or -5
        Anim.Loop("DecorKirby", s, { Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2] + rise), Rotation = idx % 2 == 0 and 20 or -20 }, 1.0 + idx * 0.28, "Sine", true)
        table.insert(self.KirbyDecor, s)
    end
    -- Kirby peeks from right side of topbar
    local peekHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "KirbyPeek", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 42), ZIndex = 3, Parent = topbar })
    local peekKirby = Sprite.New(peekHolder, "kirby_body", 22)
    peekKirby.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    peekKirby.Position    = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 6)
    Anim.Loop("DecorKirby", peekHolder, { Position = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0, 4) }, 1.6, "Sine", true)
    self.KirbyPeek = peekHolder
    table.insert(self.KirbyDecor, peekHolder)

    -- Bottom accent: pink cloud-puff band (Dream Land horizon)
    local cloudBand = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "KirbyCloudBand", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1, Parent = self.Body })
    Draw.Corner(cloudBand, Config.Window.Radius)
    local bandGrad = Draw.New("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.68) }), Parent = cloudBand })
    Theme.OnRender(cloudBand, function()
        cloudBand.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Colors.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 180)
    end)
    Anim.Loop("DecorKirbyBody", bandGrad, { Offset = Vector2.new(0, 0.1) }, 2.8, "Sine", true)
    self.KirbyBand = cloudBand
    table.insert(self.KirbyBody, cloudBand)

    -- Meta Knight floats on safe left edge
    local mkHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "KirbyMK", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.50, 0, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 36), ZIndex = 3, Parent = topbar })
    local mk = Sprite.New(mkHolder, "kirby_metaknight", 18)
    mk.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    mk.Position    = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 6)
    Anim.Loop("DecorKirby", mkHolder, { Position = UDim2.new(0.50, 0, 0, 10), Rotation = -6 }, 2.1, "Sine", true)
    self.KirbyMK = mkHolder
    table.insert(self.KirbyDecor, mkHolder)
end

function Window:BuildPokemonDecor(topbar)
    self.PokemonDecor = {}
    self.PokemonBody = {}
    local boltSpots = {
        { 0.53, 12, 14 }, { 0.58, 24, 12 }, { 0.63, 8, 16 },
        { 0.68, 22, 12 }, { 0.73, 14, 14 },
    }
    for idx, spot in ipairs(boltSpots) do
        local boltHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2]), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(spot[3], spot[3]), ZIndex = 3, Parent = topbar })
        local b1 = Draw.New("Frame", { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.2, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.6, 0), Rotation = -25, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = boltHolder })
        local b2 = Draw.New("Frame", { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.4, 0.4), Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.6, 0), Rotation = -25, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = boltHolder })
        Theme.Bind(b1, { BackgroundColor3 = "Coin" })
        Theme.Bind(b2, { BackgroundColor3 = "Accent" })
        Anim.Loop("DecorPokemon", boltHolder, { Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], 6, 0, spot[2] - 4), Rotation = 15 }, 0.5 + idx * 0.2, "Sine", true)
        table.insert(self.PokemonDecor, boltHolder)
    end
    -- Pikachu mascot peeking in topbar
    local pikaHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "PokePika", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 38), ZIndex = 3, Parent = topbar })
    local pika = Sprite.New(pikaHolder, "poke_pikachu", 22)
    pika.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    pika.Position    = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 4)
    Anim.Loop("DecorPokemon", pikaHolder, { Position = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0, 10), Rotation = 4 }, 1.4, "Sine", true)
    self.PikachuPeek = pikaHolder
    table.insert(self.PokemonDecor, pikaHolder)
    -- Steaming cup on safe left edge
    local cupHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "PokeCup", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.50, 0, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 36), ZIndex = 3, Parent = topbar })
    local cup = Sprite.New(cupHolder, "poke_cup", 20)
    cup.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    cup.Position    = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 4)
    local steam = Draw.New("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 12), BackgroundTransparency = 0.4, ZIndex = 4, Parent = cupHolder })
    Draw.Corner(steam, 2)
    Theme.Bind(steam, { BackgroundColor3 = "White" })
    Anim.Loop("DecorPokemon", steam, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11, -6), BackgroundTransparency = 0.95 }, 1.2, "Sine", true)
    Anim.Loop("DecorPokemon", cup, { Rotation = 4 }, 1.8, "Sine", true)
    self.PokeCup = cupHolder
    table.insert(self.PokemonDecor, cupHolder)
    -- Bottom accent: café warm amber gradient band with PokéBall rolling
    local cafeBand = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "PokeCafeBand", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1, Parent = self.Body })
    Draw.Corner(cafeBand, Config.Window.Radius)
    local bandGrad = Draw.New("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.72) }), Parent = cafeBand })
    Theme.OnRender(cafeBand, function()
        cafeBand.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Colors.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(232, 86, 60)
    end)
    Anim.Loop("DecorPokemonBody", bandGrad, { Offset = Vector2.new(0, 0.08) }, 3.0, "Sine", true)
    -- PokéBall rolling along the bottom
    local ballHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "PokeBall", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 1, -38), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), ZIndex = 4, Parent = self.Body })
    local ball = Sprite.New(ballHolder, "poke_ball", 24)
    ball.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    ball.Position    = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 13)
    Anim.Loop("DecorPokemonBody", ball, { Rotation = 360 }, 2.0, "Linear", false)
    Anim.Loop("DecorPokemonBody", ballHolder, { Position = UDim2.new(0.85, 0, 1, -38) }, 6.0, "Sine", true)
    self.PokeBand = cafeBand
    self.PokeBallRoll = ballHolder
    table.insert(self.PokemonBody, cafeBand)
    table.insert(self.PokemonBody, ballHolder)
end

function Window:BuildAnimalCrossingDecor(topbar)
    self.AnimalCrossingDecor = {}
    self.AnimalCrossingBody = {}
    local leafSpots = {
        { 0.52, 10, "ac_leaf", 18 }, { 0.57, 24, "ac_cherry", 16 }, { 0.62, 8,  "ac_leaf", 20 },
        { 0.67, 20, "ac_cherry", 16 }, { 0.72, 12, "ac_leaf", 18 },
    }
    for idx, spot in ipairs(leafSpots) do
        local leaf = Sprite.New(topbar, spot[3], spot[4])
        leaf.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        leaf.Position    = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2])
        leaf.ZIndex      = 2
        local sway = idx % 2 == 0 and 8 or -6
        Anim.Loop("DecorAC", leaf, { Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], sway, 0, spot[2] + 4), Rotation = idx % 2 == 0 and 12 or -10 }, 2.4 + idx * 0.45, "Sine", true)
        table.insert(self.AnimalCrossingDecor, leaf)
    end
    -- Isabelle mascot sitting at desk
    local isabelleHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "ACIsabelle", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 38), ZIndex = 3, Parent = topbar })
    local isabelle = Sprite.New(isabelleHolder, "ac_isabelle", 22)
    isabelle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    isabelle.Position    = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 4)
    Anim.Loop("DecorAC", isabelleHolder, { Position = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0, 10) }, 2.0, "Sine", true)
    self.ACIsabelle = isabelleHolder
    table.insert(self.AnimalCrossingDecor, isabelleHolder)
    -- Tom Nook holds a bell on safe left edge
    local nookHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "ACNook", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.50, 0, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 36), ZIndex = 3, Parent = topbar })
    local nook = Sprite.New(nookHolder, "ac_tomnook", 20)
    nook.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    nook.Position    = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4)
    Anim.Loop("DecorAC", nookHolder, { Rotation = 8 }, 1.2, "Sine", true)
    self.ACNook = nookHolder
    table.insert(self.AnimalCrossingDecor, nookHolder)
    -- Bottom accent: island grass green band with flower pips
    local grassBand = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "ACGrassBand", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1, Parent = self.Body })
    Draw.Corner(grassBand, Config.Window.Radius)
    local bandGrad = Draw.New("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.70) }), Parent = grassBand })
    Theme.OnRender(grassBand, function()
        grassBand.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Colors.Grass or Color3.fromRGB(154, 205, 123)
    end)
    for i = 1, 7 do
        local spriteKey = (i % 2 == 0) and "ac_leaf" or "ac_cherry"
        local pip = Sprite.New(grassBand, spriteKey, 14)
        pip.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        pip.Position    = UDim2.new((i-1)/8, 6, 0.5, 0)
        pip.ZIndex      = 3
        Anim.Loop("DecorACBody", pip, { Position = UDim2.new((i-1)/8, 6, 0.5, -2) }, 1.5 + i * 0.3, "Sine", true)
    end
    self.ACBand = grassBand
    table.insert(self.AnimalCrossingBody, grassBand)
end

function Window:BuildStardewDecor(topbar)
    self.StardewDecor = {}
    self.StardewBody = {}
    local junimoSpots = {
        { 0.52, 10, "sdv_junimo", 20 }, { 0.57, 22, "sdv_stardrop", 18 }, { 0.62, 8,  "sdv_junimo", 20 },
        { 0.67, 20, "sdv_gold",   16 }, { 0.72, 12, "sdv_junimo",   20 },
    }
    for idx, spot in ipairs(junimoSpots) do
        local s = Sprite.New(topbar, spot[3], spot[4])
        s.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        s.Position    = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2])
        s.ZIndex      = 2
        local hop = idx % 2 == 0 and 7 or -5
        Anim.Loop("DecorSDV", s, { Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2] + hop), Rotation = idx % 2 == 0 and 10 or -8 }, 1.2 + idx * 0.32, "Sine", true)
        table.insert(self.StardewDecor, s)
    end
    -- Farmer stands on safe right edge
    local farmerHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "SDVFarmer", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 38), ZIndex = 3, Parent = topbar })
    local farmer = Sprite.New(farmerHolder, "sdv_farmer", 20)
    farmer.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    farmer.Position    = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4)
    Anim.Loop("DecorSDV", farmerHolder, { Position = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0, 9) }, 1.8, "Sine", true)
    self.SDVFarmer = farmerHolder
    table.insert(self.StardewDecor, farmerHolder)
    -- Wizard floats on safe left edge
    local wizardHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "SDVWizard", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.50, 0, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 36), ZIndex = 3, Parent = topbar })
    local wizard = Sprite.New(wizardHolder, "sdv_wizard", 20)
    wizard.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    wizard.Position    = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4)
    Anim.Loop("DecorSDV", wizardHolder, { Position = UDim2.new(0.50, 0, 0, 12), Rotation = -5 }, 2.2, "Sine", true)
    self.SDVWizard = wizardHolder
    table.insert(self.StardewDecor, wizardHolder)
    -- Bottom accent: wooden plank strip — farm-field rows
    local plankBand = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "SDVPlankBand", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 56), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1, Parent = self.Body })
    Draw.Corner(plankBand, Config.Window.Radius)
    local bandGrad = Draw.New("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.65), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.55) }), Parent = plankBand })
    Theme.OnRender(plankBand, function()
        plankBand.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Colors.Brick or Color3.fromRGB(168, 132, 74)
    end)
    for i = 1, 7 do
        local plank = Draw.New("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(i/8, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, ZIndex = 2, Parent = plankBand })
        Theme.OnRender(plank, function()
            plank.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Colors.BrickDark or Color3.fromRGB(122, 92, 48)
        end)
    end
    local cropIcons = { "sdv_seed", "sdv_can", "sdv_seed", "sdv_gold", "sdv_seed" }
    for i, icon in ipairs(cropIcons) do
        local iconSprite = Sprite.New(plankBand, icon, 16)
        iconSprite.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        iconSprite.Position    = UDim2.new((i-1)/5 + 0.05, 0, 0.5, 0)
        iconSprite.ZIndex      = 3
        Anim.Loop("DecorSDVBody", iconSprite, { Position = UDim2.new((i-1)/5 + 0.05, 0, 0.5, -3) }, 1.6 + i * 0.4, "Sine", true)
    end
    -- Stardrop corner sparkles on the window frame
    local starDropGlow = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "SDVGlow", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-3, -3), Size = UDim2.new(1, 6 - Config.Window.Shadow, 1, 6 - Config.Window.Shadow), ZIndex = 0, Parent = self.Pop })
    Draw.Corner(starDropGlow, Config.Window.Radius + 3)
    local sdvStroke = Draw.New("UIStroke", { Thickness = 3, Transparency = 0.35, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = starDropGlow })
    Theme.OnRender(sdvStroke, function()
        sdvStroke.Color = Theme.Colors.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(212, 100, 28)
    end)
    Anim.Loop("DecorSDVGlow", sdvStroke, { Transparency = 0.80, Thickness = 1 }, 2.4, "Sine", true)
    self.SDVBand  = plankBand
    self.SDVGlow  = starDropGlow
    table.insert(self.StardewBody, plankBand)
    table.insert(self.StardewBody, starDropGlow)
end

function Window:BuildTamagotchiDecor(topbar)
    self.TamagotchiDecor = {}
    self.TamagotchiBody = {}
    local tamaSpots = {
        { 0.52, 10, "tama_babytchi", 18 }, { 0.57, 24, "tama_mametchi",   20 }, { 0.62, 8,  "tama_kuchipatchi", 18 },
        { 0.67, 22, "tama_memetchi", 18 }, { 0.72, 10, "tama_babytchi",   18 },
    }
    for idx, spot in ipairs(tamaSpots) do
        local s = Sprite.New(topbar, spot[3], spot[4])
        s.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        s.Position    = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2])
        s.ZIndex      = 2
        local hop = idx % 2 == 0 and 8 or -6
        Anim.Loop("DecorTama", s, { Position = UDim2.new(spot[1], 0, 0, spot[2] + hop) }, 0.8 + idx * 0.25, "Bounce", true)
        table.insert(self.TamagotchiDecor, s)
    end
    -- Tama egg bouncing prominently
    local eggHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "TamaEgg", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0, 3), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 38), ZIndex = 3, Parent = topbar })
    local egg = Sprite.New(eggHolder, "tama_egg", 22)
    egg.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    egg.Position    = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 4)
    Anim.Loop("DecorTama", eggHolder, { Position = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0, 12) }, 0.7, "Bounce", true)
    self.TamaEgg = eggHolder
    table.insert(self.TamagotchiDecor, eggHolder)
    -- Tamagotchi device outer bezel
    local bezel = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "TamaBezel", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-5, -5), Size = UDim2.new(1, 10 - Config.Window.Shadow, 1, 10 - Config.Window.Shadow), ZIndex = 0, Parent = self.Pop })
    Draw.Corner(bezel, 8)
    local bezelStroke = Draw.New("UIStroke", { Thickness = 5, Transparency = 0.2, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = bezel })
    Theme.OnRender(bezelStroke, function()
        bezelStroke.Color = Theme.Colors.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 126, 176)
    end)
    Anim.Loop("DecorTamaGlow", bezelStroke, { Transparency = 0.65, Thickness = 3 }, 1.4, "Sine", true)
    self.TamaBezel = bezel
    table.insert(self.TamagotchiBody, bezel)
    -- Speaker grill dots
    local grillHolder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "TamaGrill", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.new(1, -Config.Window.Shadow - 10, 1, -8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 10), ZIndex = 5, Parent = self.Body })
    for d = 1, 5 do
        local dot = Draw.New("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new((d-1)/5 + 0.1, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5, Parent = grillHolder })
        Draw.Corner(dot, 3)
        Theme.OnRender(dot, function()
            dot.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Colors.Outline or Color3.fromRGB(32, 72, 112)
            dot.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
        end)
    end
    self.TamaGrill = grillHolder
    table.insert(self.TamagotchiBody, grillHolder)
    -- LCD scanline overlay
    local scanlines = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "TamaScanlines", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = self.Body })
    for row = 0, 60 do
        local line = Draw.New("Frame", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, row * 10 + 4), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 0.88, ZIndex = 0, Parent = scanlines })
        Theme.OnRender(line, function()
            line.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Colors.Outline or Color3.fromRGB(32, 72, 112)
        end)
    end
    self.TamaScanlines = scanlines
    table.insert(self.TamagotchiBody, scanlines)
    -- Bottom accent: LCD beige band
    local lcdBand = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "TamaLCDBand", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 1, Parent = self.Body })
    Draw.Corner(lcdBand, Config.Window.Radius)
    local bandGrad = Draw.New("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.65) }), Parent = lcdBand })
    Theme.OnRender(lcdBand, function()
        lcdBand.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Colors.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 126, 176)
    end)
    for i = 1, 5 do
        local heart = Sprite.New(lcdBand, "tama_heart", 14)
        heart.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        heart.Position    = UDim2.new((i-1)/6 + 0.05, 0, 0.5, 0)
        heart.ZIndex      = 3
        Anim.Loop("DecorTamaBody", heart, { Position = UDim2.new((i-1)/6 + 0.05, 0, 0.5, -3) }, 0.9 + i * 0.25, "Sine", true)
    end
    self.TamaLCDBand = lcdBand
    table.insert(self.TamagotchiBody, lcdBand)
end

function Window:RenderDecor()
    local wide = self.Size and self.Size.X >= 640
    local decor = Theme.Colors.Decor
    local shown = self.Visible ~= false
    local expanded = shown and not self.Minimized

    -- Overworld / Standard
    local showClouds  = shown and wide and (decor == "Clouds" or decor == "Overworld")
    local showStars   = shown and wide and decor == "Stars"

    -- Individual themes
    local showHalloween    = shown and wide and decor == "Halloween"
    local showSpookyBody   = expanded and decor == "Halloween"
    local showKirby        = shown and wide and decor == "Kirby"
    local showKirbyBody    = expanded and decor == "Kirby"
    local showPokemon      = shown and wide and decor == "PokemonCafe"
    local showPokemonBody  = expanded and decor == "PokemonCafe"
    local showAC           = shown and wide and decor == "AnimalCrossing"
    local showACBody       = expanded and decor == "AnimalCrossing"
    local showSDV          = shown and wide and decor == "StardewValley"
    local showSDVBody      = expanded and decor == "StardewValley"
    local showTama         = shown and wide and decor == "Tamagotchi"
    local showTamaBody     = expanded and decor == "Tamagotchi"
    local showCastle       = shown and wide and decor == "Castle"
    local showUnderground  = shown and wide and decor == "Underground"
    local showIce          = shown and wide and (decor == "IceLand" or decor == "Snow")
    local showDesert       = shown and wide and decor == "Desert"
    local showGhostHouse   = shown and wide and decor == "GhostHouse"
    local showStarRoad     = shown and wide and decor == "StarRoad"
    local showJungle       = shown and wide and decor == "Jungle"
    local showSkyWorld     = shown and wide and decor == "SkyWorld"

    for _, c in ipairs(self.Clouds or {}) do c.Visible = showClouds end
    for _, s in ipairs(self.Stars or {}) do s.Visible = showStars end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.Spooky or {}) do item.Visible = showHalloween end
    for _, part in ipairs(self.SpookyBody or {}) do part.Visible = showSpookyBody end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.KirbyDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showKirby end
    for _, part in ipairs(self.KirbyBody or {}) do part.Visible = showKirbyBody end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.PokemonDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showPokemon end
    for _, part in ipairs(self.PokemonBody or {}) do part.Visible = showPokemonBody end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.AnimalCrossingDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showAC end
    for _, part in ipairs(self.AnimalCrossingBody or {}) do part.Visible = showACBody end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.StardewDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showSDV end
    for _, part in ipairs(self.StardewBody or {}) do part.Visible = showSDVBody end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.TamagotchiDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showTama end
    for _, part in ipairs(self.TamagotchiBody or {}) do part.Visible = showTamaBody end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.CastleDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showCastle end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.UndergroundDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showUnderground end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.IceDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showIce end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.DesertDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showDesert end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.GhostHouseDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showGhostHouse end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.StarRoadDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showStarRoad end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.JungleDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showJungle end
    for _, item in ipairs(self.SkyWorldDecor or {}) do item.Visible = showSkyWorld end

    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorClouds", showClouds)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorStars", showStars)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorSpooky", showHalloween)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorSpookyBody", showSpookyBody)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorKirby", showKirby)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorKirbyBody", showKirbyBody)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorPokemon", showPokemon)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorPokemonBody", showPokemonBody)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorAC", showAC)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorACBody", showACBody)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorSDV", showSDV)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorSDVBody", showSDVBody)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorSDVGlow", showSDVBody)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorTama", showTama)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorTamaBody", showTamaBody)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorTamaGlow", showTamaBody)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorCastle", showCastle)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorUnderground", showUnderground)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorIce", showIce)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorDesert", showDesert)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorGhostHouse", showGhostHouse)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorStarRoad", showStarRoad)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorJungle", showJungle)
    Anim.SetGroupActive("DecorSkyWorld", showSkyWorld)
end

function Window:BuildTitle(parent)
    local holder = Draw.New("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 34), LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = parent })
    Draw.List(holder, 1, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)
    self.Letters = {}
    local size = Config.Text.Title
    local index = 0
    local rawTitle = tostring(Lang.Resolve(self.Title) or self.Title or "")
    for char in rawTitle:upper():gmatch(utf8.charpattern) do
        local bounds = Layout.Measure(char, size, "Logo", 200)
        local slot = Draw.New("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(char == " " and size * 0.3 or bounds.X, size + 4), LayoutOrder = #self.Letters + 1, Parent = holder })
        local letter = Draw.Text({ Text = char, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = slot }, "Logo", size, Config.TitleColors[index % #Config.TitleColors + 1])
        Draw.Stroke(letter, "Ink", 2)
        table.insert(self.Letters, letter)
        index += char == " " and 0 or 1
    end
    self.TitleHolder = holder
end

function Window:WaveTitle()
    for index, letter in ipairs(self.Letters) do
        letter.Position = UDim2.new()
        Anim.Tween(letter, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, -6) }, 0.14, "Out", 0, true, index * 0.045)
    end
end

function Window:BuildPill(parent, text)
    local pill = Draw.Box("Frame", { AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22), LayoutOrder = 2, Parent = parent }, "Shadow", nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
    pill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    Draw.Padding(pill, 0, 10, 0, 10)
    Draw.Text({ AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Parent = pill }, "Body", Util.TextSize("Small"), "TopbarText", text)
    pill.Visible = text ~= ""
    return pill
end

function Window:BuildLanguagePill(parent)
    local pill = Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 28), LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = parent }, "Shadow", "Outline", UDim.new(1, 0), 2)
    pill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    local segments = {}
    for index, code in ipairs({ "EN", "TH" }) do
        local segment = Draw.Box("TextButton", { Position = UDim2.new((index - 1) * 0.5, 2, 0, 2), Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 1, -4), Parent = pill }, "Coin", nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
        local label = Draw.Text({ Text = code, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = segment }, "Body", 13, "White")
        segment.Activated:Connect(function()
            Library:SetLanguage(code)
        end)
        segments[code] = { Button = segment, Label = label }
    end
    local function Render()
        for code, segment in pairs(segments) do
            local active = State.Language == code
            Anim.Tween(segment.Button, { BackgroundTransparency = active and 0 or 1 }, Config.Tween.Fast)
            Theme.Bind(segment.Label, { TextColor3 = active and "Ink" or "TopbarText" })
        end
    end
    Lang.OnChange(pill, Render)
    Render()
end

-- Draw close icon with Frame lines for consistent cross-device rendering
function Window:BuildTopButton(parent, kind, face, shade, ink, callback)
    local size = State.Touch and 36 or 30
    local holder, top = Draw.Block(parent, face, shade, 8, 3)
    holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size + 1)
    holder.LayoutOrder = 2 + #parent:GetChildren()
    local strokes = kind == "Close" and { 45, -45 } or { 0 }
    for _, rotation in ipairs(strokes) do
        Draw.Box("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 3), Rotation = rotation, Parent = top }, ink, nil, 2)
    end
    local hit = Draw.New("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 3, Parent = holder })
    hit.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        Anim.Tween(top, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 2) }, 0.06, "Linear")
    end)
    hit.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
        Anim.Tween(top, { Position = UDim2.new() }, Config.Tween.Normal, "Back")
    end)
    hit.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            Anim.Tween(top, { Position = UDim2.new() }, Config.Tween.Normal, "Back")
        end
    end)
    hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        Anim.Tween(top, { Position = UDim2.new() }, Config.Tween.Fast)
    end)
    hit.Activated:Connect(function()
        Fx.Ripple(top, nil, ink)
        Fx.Squash(holder, 1.2)
        if callback then
            callback()
        end
    end)
    return holder, top
end

function Window:BuildSidebar()
    local top = Config.Window.Topbar
    local sidebar = Draw.Box("Frame", { Name = "Sidebar", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, top), Parent = self.Body }, "Sidebar")
    Draw.Box("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, 0), ZIndex = 3, Parent = sidebar }, "Outline")
    Draw.Box("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -3, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 5, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0, Parent = sidebar }, "SidebarAlt")
    self.Sidebar = sidebar
    local scroll = Layout.ScrollFrame({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 8), Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -(Config.Window.UserCard + 8)), Parent = sidebar })
    scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    self.TabScroll = scroll
    self.TabList = Container.New(scroll, { PadX = 8, PadY = 2, GapY = 4, Window = self })
    self:BuildUserCard(sidebar)
end

function Window:BuildUserCard(sidebar)
    local card = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "User", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, Config.Window.UserCard), Parent = sidebar })
    self.UserCard = card
    Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5, Parent = card }, "SidebarAlt")
    local avatar = Draw.Box("ImageLabel", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 36), Parent = card }, "PanelHeader", "Outline", UDim.new(1, 0), 2)
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, image = pcall(Players.GetUserThumbnailAsync, Players, LocalPlayer.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
        if ok then
            avatar.Image = image
        end
    end)
    self.UserName = Draw.Text({ Text = LocalPlayer.DisplayName, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 11), Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 18), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = card }, "Body", 14, "SidebarText")
    self.UserTag = Draw.Text({ Text = "@" .. LocalPlayer.Name, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 29), Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 16), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = card }, "Desc", 12, "SidebarMuted")
    self.Avatar = avatar
end

function Window:BuildMain()
    local header = Config.Window.Header
    local main = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "Main", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, Config.Window.Topbar), Parent = self.Body })
    self.Main = main
    self.HeaderTitle = Draw.Text({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 10), Size = UDim2.new(1, -230, 0, 26), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = main }, "Display", Config.Text.Header, "Text")
    self.HeaderDesc = Draw.Text({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 36), Size = UDim2.new(1, -230, 0, 16), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = main }, "Desc", Util.TextSize("Desc") + 1, "SubText")
    local field = Draw.Box("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, header / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, Util.Metric("Box")), Parent = main }, "Element", "Outline", UDim.new(1, 0), 2)
    local icon = Icons.Vector(field, "search", 14, "SubText")
    icon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0)
    local clearBtn = Draw.Box("TextButton", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), Visible = false, Parent = field }, "Hover", nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
    Icons.Vector(clearBtn, "close", 10, "SubText").Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4)
    self.SearchClear = clearBtn
    self.Search = Draw.Text({ ClassName = "TextBox", Text = "", ClearTextOnFocus = false, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -52, 1, 0), Parent = field }, "Body", Util.TextSize("Label"), "Text")
    Lang.Bind(self.Search, Lang.Strings.Search, "PlaceholderText")
    Theme.Bind(self.Search, { PlaceholderColor3 = "Muted" })
    self.SearchField = field
    clearBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
        self.Search.Text = ""
    end)
    self.Search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        self.Query = self.Search.Text:lower()
        clearBtn.Visible = self.Query ~= ""
        self:ApplyFilter()
    end)
    self.PageHost = Draw.New("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, header), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -header), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 2, Parent = main })
    local emptyBox = Draw.Box("Frame", { Name = "EmptySearch", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.45), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 74), Visible = false, ZIndex = 5, Parent = self.PageHost }, "Panel", "Outline", 10, 2)
    local booSprite = Sprite.New(emptyBox, "boo", 24)
    booSprite.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    booSprite.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 10)
    Draw.Text({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 40), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 24), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = emptyBox }, "Body", Util.TextSize("Label"), "Muted", Lang.Strings.NoResults)
    self.EmptyState = emptyBox
    Particles.Build(main)
end

function Window:BuildGround()
    local height, radius = Config.Window.Ground, Config.Window.Radius
    local ground = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "Ground", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 3, Parent = self.Body })
    Draw.Corner(ground, radius)
    self.Ground = ground

    local content = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "GroundContent", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = ground })
    self.GroundContent = content

    local gripSize = State.Touch and 34 or 24
    local grip = Draw.New("TextButton", { Name = "Grip", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(gripSize, gripSize), ZIndex = 7, Parent = ground })
    local gripIcon = Icons.Vector(grip, "grip", 14, "White")
    gripIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
    gripIcon.Position = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2)
    self.Grip = grip
    Gui.Draggable(grip, function(begin, delta)
        if not delta then
            return { Size = self.Size, Position = self.Root.Position }
        end
        self:ResizeFrom(begin, Vector2.new(delta.X, delta.Y))
        return begin
    end)

    self:RenderGround()
    Theme.OnRender(ground, function()
        self:RenderGround()
    end)
end

function Window:RenderGround()
    if not self.GroundContent then return end
    self.GroundContent:ClearAllChildren()
    local container = self.GroundContent
    local theme = State.ThemeName or "Overworld"

    if theme == "Animal Crossing" then

function Window:ResizeFrom(begin, delta)
    if self.Minimized then
        return
    end
    self.Desired = begin.Size + delta / State.UserScale
    self:FitViewport()
    local grown = (self.Size.X - begin.Size.X) * State.UserScale
    self.Root.Position = begin.Position + UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(grown / 2), 0)
    self:ClampPosition()
end

function Window:MinSize()
    if State.Touch then
        return Config.Window.TouchMinWidth, Config.Window.TouchMinHeight
    end
    return Config.Window.MinWidth, Config.Window.MinHeight
end

function Window:ClampPosition()
    if not State.Gui or not self.Root then
        return
    end
    local viewport = State.Gui.AbsoluteSize
    if viewport.X <= 0 or viewport.Y <= 0 then
        return
    end
    local scaleFactor = math.max(0.5, State.UserScale or 1)
    local halfW = math.floor((self.Size.X * scaleFactor) / 2)
    local topH = math.floor(Config.Window.Topbar * scaleFactor)
    local pos = self.Root.Position
    local absX = viewport.X * pos.X.Scale + pos.X.Offset
    local absY = viewport.Y * pos.Y.Scale + pos.Y.Offset
    local minX = math.min(halfW + 6, viewport.X / 2)
    local maxX = math.max(minX, viewport.X - halfW - 6)
    local clampedX = math.clamp(absX, minX, maxX)
    local clampedY = math.clamp(absY, 6, math.max(6, viewport.Y - topH - 6))
    self.Root.Position = UDim2.new(pos.X.Scale, math.floor(clampedX - viewport.X * pos.X.Scale + 0.5), pos.Y.Scale, math.floor(clampedY - viewport.Y * pos.Y.Scale + 0.5))
end

function Window:FitViewport()
    local viewport = State.Gui.AbsoluteSize
    if viewport.X <= 0 then
        return
    end
    local margin = (State.Touch and Config.Window.TouchMargin or Config.Window.Margin) * 2
    local baseMinW, baseMinH = self:MinSize()
    local scaleFactor = math.max(0.5, State.UserScale or 1)
    local availW = math.max(260, (viewport.X - margin) / scaleFactor)
    local availH = math.max(220, (viewport.Y - margin) / scaleFactor)
    local minWidth = math.min(baseMinW, availW)
    local minHeight = math.min(baseMinH, availH)
    self.Size = Vector2.new(
        math.floor(math.clamp(self.Desired.X, minWidth, availW)),
        math.floor(math.clamp(self.Desired.Y, minHeight, availH))
    )
    self:ApplyLayout()
    self:ClampPosition()
    if Float.Clamp then
        Float.Clamp()
    end
end

function Window:ApplyLayout()
    local width, height = self.Size.X, self.Size.Y
    local shadow = Config.Window.Shadow
    self.Compact = width < Config.Window.CompactBreakpoint
    local sidebar = self.Compact and Config.Window.SidebarCompact or Config.Window.Sidebar
    local bodyHeight = height - Config.Window.Topbar - Config.Window.Ground
    self.Root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width + shadow, (self.Minimized and Config.Window.Topbar or height) + shadow)
    if not self.Placed then
        self.Placed = true
        self.Root.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -math.floor((height + shadow) * State.UserScale / 2))
    end
    self.UserScale.Scale = State.UserScale
    self.Sidebar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sidebar, bodyHeight)
    self.Main.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sidebar, Config.Window.Topbar)
    self.Main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width - sidebar, bodyHeight)
    self.TabList:SetWidth(sidebar - 8 - 3)
    local pageWidth = width - sidebar
    for _, tab in ipairs(self.Tabs) do
        tab.PendingWidth = pageWidth
        tab:ApplyCompact(self.Compact)
    end
    if self.ActiveTab then
        self.ActiveTab:ApplyWidth()
    end
    for _, section in ipairs(self.Sections or {}) do
        section.Hidden = self.Compact
    end
    self.TabList:MarkDirty()
    self:ApplyChrome(width, height)
end

-- Compact landscape mode: hide user card and tab subtitle to maximize content height
function Window:ApplyShort(height)
    local short = height < 430
    local header = short and 46 or Config.Window.Header
    self.UserCard.Visible = not short
    self.TabScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -(short and 8 or Config.Window.UserCard + 8))
    self.HeaderDesc.Visible = not short
    self.HeaderTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, short and 11 or 10)
    self.SearchField.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, header / 2)
    self.PageHost.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, header)
    self.PageHost.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -header)
end

function Window:ApplyChrome(width, height)
    self:ApplyShort(height)
    local narrow = width < 460
    self.SubtitlePill.Visible = self.SubTitle ~= "" and width >= 700
    self:RenderDecor()
    self.Emblem.Visible = width >= 380
    self.TitleHolder.Visible = width >= 340
    local searchWidth = self.Compact and (narrow and 110 or 140) or 200
    self.SearchField.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(searchWidth, Util.Metric("Box"))
    self.HeaderTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(searchWidth + 44), 0, 26)
    self.HeaderDesc.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(searchWidth + 44), 0, 16)
    self.UserName.Visible = not self.Compact
    self.UserTag.Visible = not self.Compact
    self.Avatar.Position = UDim2.new(0, self.Compact and 10 or 12, 0.5, 0)
end

function Window:AddTabSection(text)
    local label = Draw.Text({ Name = "Section" }, "Body", Config.Text.Section - 1, "SidebarMuted")
    Lang.Bind(label, text, "Text", string.upper)
    Draw.Padding(label, 6, 0, 0, 6)
    local item = self.TabList:Add(label, { Height = 24 })
    self.Sections = self.Sections or {}
    table.insert(self.Sections, item)
    item.Hidden = self.Compact
    return item
end

function Window:AddTab(name, icon, description)
    if type(name) == "table" and not name.EN and not name.TH then
        icon = name.Icon or icon
        description = name.Description or name.Desc or description
        name = name.Title or name.Name or name.Text or "Tab"
    end
    local tab = Tab.New(self, name, icon, description)
    table.insert(self.Tabs, tab)
    self:ApplyLayout()
    if not self.ActiveTab then
        self:SelectTab(tab)
    end
    return tab
end

function Window:SelectTab(tab)
    if type(tab) == "number" then
        tab = self.Tabs[tab]
    elseif type(tab) == "string" then
        for _, candidate in ipairs(self.Tabs) do
            if Lang.Resolve(candidate.Name) == tab or candidate.Name == tab then
                tab = candidate
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if type(tab) ~= "table" or tab == self.ActiveTab then
        return
    end
    Popup.Close()
    local previous = self.ActiveTab
    self.ActiveTab = tab
    if previous then
        previous.Page.Visible = false
        previous:RenderButton()
    end
    tab:ApplyWidth()
    tab.Page.Visible = true
    tab.Page.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 18)
    Anim.Tween(tab.Page, { Position = UDim2.new() }, Config.Tween.Slide)
    tab:RenderButton()
    Anim.Bump(tab.IconSlot, 6)
    Lang.Bind(self.HeaderTitle, tab.Name)
    Lang.Bind(self.HeaderDesc, tab.Description or "")
    local titleY = self.HeaderDesc.Visible and 10 or 11
    self.HeaderTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, titleY)
    Anim.Tween(self.HeaderTitle, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, titleY) }, Config.Tween.Slide, "Back")
    if (tab.FilteredQuery or "") ~= self.Query then
        self:ApplyFilter()
    elseif self.EmptyState then
        self.EmptyState.Visible = false
    end
    local cards = {}
    for _, box in ipairs(tab.Groupboxes) do
        if not box.Item.Filtered and not box.Item.Hidden then
            table.insert(cards, box.Card)
        end
    end
    Fx.StaggerRise(cards)
end

function Window:ApplyFilter()
    local tab = self.ActiveTab
    if not tab then
        return
    end
    local query = self.Query
    tab.FilteredQuery = query
    local visibleCount = 0
    local columnChanged = false
    for _, box in ipairs(tab.Groupboxes) do
        local titleMatch = query == "" or box.SearchTitle:find(query, 1, true) ~= nil
        local anyMatch = false
        local boxChanged = false
        for _, entry in ipairs(box.Items) do
            local filtered = not titleMatch and (entry.Search == nil or not entry.Search:find(query, 1, true))
            if entry.Filtered ~= filtered then
                entry.Filtered = filtered
                boxChanged = true
            end
            anyMatch = anyMatch or (entry.Search ~= nil and not filtered)
        end
        local boxFiltered = not titleMatch and not anyMatch
        if box.Item.Filtered ~= boxFiltered then
            box.Item.Filtered = boxFiltered
            columnChanged = true
        end
        if not boxFiltered and not box.Item.Hidden then
            visibleCount += 1
        end
        if boxChanged then
            box:MarkDirty()
            box.Column:MarkDirty()
            columnChanged = true
        end
    end
    if columnChanged then
        tab:ApplyColumns()
    end
    if self.EmptyState then
        self.EmptyState.Visible = query ~= "" and visibleCount == 0
    end
end

function Window:SetVisible(visible)
    if visible == self.Visible then
        return
    end
    self.Visible = visible
    Popup.Close()
    if not visible then
        Dialog.Close()
        Sheet.Close()
    end
    Tooltip.Token = nil
    Tooltip.Frame.Visible = false
    if Float.Render then
        Float.Render()
    end
    self:RenderDecor()
    if visible then
        self.Root.Visible = true
        self.PopScale.Scale = 0.82
        Anim.Tween(self.PopScale, { Scale = 1 }, Config.Tween.Pop, "Back")
        self:WaveTitle()
        Particles.Resume()
        return
    end
    if Float.Button and not Float.Button.Visible and Float.State.Visible then
        Float.SetVisible(true)
    end
    local shrink = Anim.Tween(self.PopScale, { Scale = 0.82 }, 0.14, "In")
    shrink.Completed:Once(function()
        if not self.Visible then
            self.Root.Visible = false
        end
    end)
end

function Window:Toggle()
    if not self.Ready then
        return
    end
    self:SetVisible(not self.Visible)
end

function Window:SetContentVisible(visible)
    self.Sidebar.Visible, self.Main.Visible, self.Ground.Visible = visible, visible, visible
end

function Window:SetMinimized(minimized)
    minimized = minimized == true
    if self.Minimized == minimized then
        return
    end
    self.Minimized = minimized
    Popup.Close()
    Dialog.Close()
    Sheet.Close()
    self:RenderDecor()
    if not minimized then
        self:SetContentVisible(true)
        self.TopbarFill.Visible, self.TopbarLine.Visible = true, true
    end
    Anim.Tween(self.TopbarFill, { BackgroundTransparency = minimized and 1 or 0 }, Config.Tween.Fast)
    Anim.Tween(self.TopbarLine, { BackgroundTransparency = minimized and 1 or 0 }, Config.Tween.Fast)
    Anim.Tween(self.ExpandBar, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, minimized and 0.5 or 0, 0) }, Config.Tween.Normal, "Back")
    local shadow = Config.Window.Shadow
    local height = minimized and Config.Window.Topbar or self.Size.Y
    local tween = Anim.Tween(self.Root, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(self.Size.X + shadow, height + shadow) }, 0.34, "Out")
    tween.Completed:Once(function()
        if self.Minimized ~= minimized then
            return
        end
        if minimized then
            self:SetContentVisible(false)
            self.TopbarFill.Visible, self.TopbarLine.Visible = false, false
        else
            Particles.Resume()
        end
    end)
end

function Window:SetScale(scale)
    State.UserScale = math.clamp(tonumber(scale) or 1, Config.ScaleRange.Min, Config.ScaleRange.Max)
    self:FitViewport()
end

function Tab.New(window, name, icon, description)
    local resolvedIcon = (icon and icon ~= "") and icon or Icons.FromTitle(name, "main")
    local tab = setmetatable({ Window = window, Name = name, OriginalIcon = icon, Icon = resolvedIcon, Description = description, Groupboxes = {} }, Tab)
    tab:BuildButton()
    tab:BuildPage()
    return tab
end

function Tab:BuildButton()
    local height = Util.Metric("Tab")
    local holder = Draw.New("TextButton", { Name = "Tab", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
    local shade = Draw.Box("Frame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 3), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -3), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = holder }, "Shadow", nil, 10)
    local face = Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -3), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = holder }, "TabActive", nil, 10)
    local stroke = Draw.Stroke(face, "Outline", 2, true)
    stroke.Transparency = 1
    self.IconSlot = Draw.New("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24), Parent = face })
    local ic = Icons.Create(self.IconSlot, self.Icon, 24, nil, self.Name)
    if ic then
        ic.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        ic.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    end
    self.TabLabel = Draw.Text({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = face }, "Body", Util.TextSize("Label") + 1, "SidebarText")
    self.Label = self.TabLabel
    Lang.Bind(self.TabLabel, self.Name)
    self.TabButton, self.Face, self.Shade, self.Stroke = holder, face, shade, stroke
    holder.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        self.Hovered = not State.Touch
        self:RenderButton()
        if not (self.Window.ActiveTab == self) and self.IconSlot then
            Anim.Bump(self.IconSlot, 2)
        end
    end)
    holder.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        self.Hovered = false
        self:RenderButton()
    end)
    holder.Activated:Connect(function()
        self.Window:SelectTab(self)
    end)
    self.Window.TabList:Add(holder, { Height = height })
    Theme.OnRender(self, function()
        self:RenderButton(true)
        if self.IconSlot then
            self.IconSlot:ClearAllChildren()
            local activeIcon = (self.OriginalIcon and self.OriginalIcon ~= "") and self.OriginalIcon or Icons.FromTitle(self.Name, "main")
            local ic = Icons.Create(self.IconSlot, activeIcon, 24, nil, self.Name)
            ic.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            ic.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        end
    end)
    if self.Description then
        Tooltip.Attach(holder, self.Description)
    end
end

function Tab:RenderButton(instant)
    local active = self.Window.ActiveTab == self
    local duration = instant and 0 or Config.Tween.Normal
    local faceAlpha = active and 0 or (self.Hovered and 0.8 or 1)
    Anim.Tween(self.Face, { BackgroundTransparency = faceAlpha }, duration)
    Anim.Tween(self.Shade, { BackgroundTransparency = active and 0 or 1 }, duration)
    Anim.Tween(self.Stroke, { Transparency = active and 0 or 1 }, duration)
    Theme.Bind(self.TabLabel, { TextColor3 = active and "TabActiveText" or "SidebarText" })
end

function Tab:Select()
    self.Window:SelectTab(self)
end

function Tab:SetTitle(title)
    self.Name = title
    if self.TabLabel then
        Lang.Bind(self.TabLabel, title)
    end
end

-- Defer layout calculation for inactive tabs until selected
function Tab:ApplyWidth()
    local width = self.PendingWidth
    if not width then
        return
    end
    self.PendingWidth = nil
    self.Root:SetWidth(width - Config.Page.ScrollBar)
    self:ApplyColumns(width)
end

function Tab:ApplyCompact(compact)
    self.TabLabel.Visible = not compact
    self.IconSlot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(compact and 0.5 or 0, 0.5)
    self.IconSlot.Position = compact and UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) or UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, 0)
end

function Tab:BuildPage()
    local page = Layout.ScrollFrame({ Name = "Page", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Visible = false, Parent = self.Window.PageHost })
    local gap = Config.Gap.Column
    self.Page = page
    self.Root = Container.New(page, { PadX = Config.Page.Pad, PadY = 6, GapX = gap, GapY = gap, Window = self.Window, Tab = self })
    local leftHost = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "Left", BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
    local rightHost = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "Right", BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
    self.Left = Container.New(leftHost, { GapY = gap })
    self.Right = Container.New(rightHost, { GapY = gap })
    self.LeftItem = self.Root:Add(leftHost, { Width = 0.5, Height = function()
        return self.Left.ContentHeight
    end, Child = self.Left })
    self.Root:SameLine()
    self.RightItem = self.Root:Add(rightHost, { Height = function()
        return self.Right.ContentHeight
    end, Child = self.Right })
end

function Tab.ColumnHasVisible(column)
    for _, entry in ipairs(column.Items) do
        if not entry.Hidden and not entry.Filtered then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

function Tab:ApplyColumns(pageWidth)
    self.PageWidth = pageWidth or self.PageWidth or 0
    local hasLeft = Tab.ColumnHasVisible(self.Left)
    local hasRight = Tab.ColumnHasVisible(self.Right)
    local single = self.PageWidth < Config.Window.TwoColumnMin or not hasLeft or not hasRight
    self.LeftItem.Hidden = not hasLeft
    self.RightItem.Hidden = not hasRight
    self.LeftItem.Width = (not single) and 0.5 or nil
    self.RightItem.SameLine = not single
    self.Root:MarkDirty()
end

function Tab:AddGroupbox(info, side)
    if type(info) ~= "table" or info.EN then
        info = { Name = info, Side = side }
    else
        info.Name = info.Name or info.Title or info.Text or ""
    end
    local column = info.Side == "Right" and self.Right or self.Left
    local shadow, header, radius = Config.Group.Shadow, Config.Group.Header, Config.Group.Radius
    local collapsed = info.Collapsed == true
    local initOpen = collapsed and 0 or 1
    local holder = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "Groupbox", BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
    Draw.Box("Frame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(shadow, shadow), Size = UDim2.new(1, -shadow, 1, -shadow), Parent = holder }, "Shadow", nil, "Group")
    local card = Draw.Box("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -shadow, 1, -shadow), Parent = holder }, "Panel", "Outline", "Group", Config.Group.Stroke)
    local body = Draw.New("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, header), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -header), ClipsDescendants = true, Visible = not collapsed, Parent = card })
    local box = Container.New(body, { PadX = Config.Group.PadX, PadY = Config.Group.PadY, Window = self.Window, Tab = self })
    setmetatable(box, Groupbox)
    box.Column, box.Card, box.Body, box.Collapsed, box.SearchTitle = column, card, body, collapsed, Lang.SearchText(info.Name)
    box.Open = Draw.New("NumberValue", { Value = initOpen })
    box:BuildHeader(card, info)
    box.Item = column:Add(holder, {
        Height = function()
            return header + math.floor(box.ContentHeight * box.Open.Value) + shadow
        end,
        Child = box,
        ChildInset = shadow,
    })
    box.Open.Changed:Connect(function(v)
        local show = v > 0.02
        box.Body.Visible = show
        if box.HeaderPatch then
            box.HeaderPatch.Visible = show
            box.HeaderPatch.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(1 - v * 1.4, 0, 1)
        end
        if box.HeaderDivider then
            box.HeaderDivider.Visible = show
            box.HeaderDivider.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(1 - v * 1.4, 0, 1)
        end
        column:MarkDirty()
    end)
    table.insert(self.Groupboxes, box)
    self:ApplyColumns()
    return box
end

function Tab:AddLeftGroupbox(name, icon, badge)
    if type(name) == "table" and not name.EN and not name.TH then
        icon = name.Icon or icon
        badge = name.Badge or badge
        name = name.Title or name.Name or name.Text or ""
    end
    return self:AddGroupbox({ Name = name, Side = "Left", Icon = icon, Badge = badge })
end

function Tab:AddRightGroupbox(name, icon, badge)
    if type(name) == "table" and not name.EN and not name.TH then
        icon = name.Icon or icon
        badge = name.Badge or badge
        name = name.Title or name.Name or name.Text or ""
    end
    return self:AddGroupbox({ Name = name, Side = "Right", Icon = icon, Badge = badge })
end

function Tab:EnsureGroup()
    if not self.DefaultGroup then
        self.DefaultGroup = self:AddLeftGroupbox(self.Name)
    end
    return self.DefaultGroup
end

Window.Tab = Window.AddTab
Window.Section = Window.AddTabSection
function Window:Dialog(spec)
    return Dialog.Show(spec)
end
function Window:Sheet(spec)
    return Sheet.Show(spec)
end
function Window:Notify(...)
    return Library:Notify(...)
end
function Window:SetTheme(...)
    return Library:SetTheme(...)
end
function Window:SetLanguage(...)
    return Library:SetLanguage(...)
end
function Window:GetLanguage(...)
    return Library:GetLanguage(...)
end
function Window:Destroy()
    return Library:Unload()
end
function Window:Unload()
    return Library:Unload()
end

Tab.Section = Tab.AddGroupbox
Tab.AddSection = Tab.AddGroupbox
Tab.LeftGroupbox = Tab.AddLeftGroupbox
Tab.RightGroupbox = Tab.AddRightGroupbox
for _, method in ipairs({ "AddToggle", "AddCheckbox", "AddSlider", "AddDropdown", "AddInput", "AddButton", "AddKeybind", "AddColorPicker", "AddLabel", "AddParagraph", "AddStepper", "AddRangeSlider", "AddSegmented", "AddStat", "AddStatus", "AddTable", "AddMonitor", "AddStatGrid", "AddLiveGraph", "AddInventoryGrid", "Toggle", "Checkbox", "Slider", "Dropdown", "Input", "Button", "Keybind", "Colorpicker", "Label", "Paragraph", "Monitor", "StatGrid", "LiveGraph", "InventoryGrid" }) do
    Tab[method] = function(tabSelf, ...)
        local grp = tabSelf:EnsureGroup()
        return grp[method](grp, ...)
    end
end

function Groupbox:BuildHeader(card, info)
    local height, radius = Config.Group.Header, Config.Group.Radius
    local collapsed = info.Collapsed == true
    local bar = Draw.Box("TextButton", { Name = "Header", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height), Parent = card }, "PanelHeader", nil, "Group")
    self.HeaderPatch = Draw.Box("Frame", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -radius), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, radius), Visible = not collapsed, BackgroundTransparency = collapsed and 1 or 0, Parent = bar }, "PanelHeader")
    self.HeaderDivider = Draw.Box("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), ZIndex = 2, Visible = not collapsed, BackgroundTransparency = collapsed and 1 or 0, Parent = bar }, "Outline")
    local iconSlot = Draw.New("Frame", { Name = "GroupIconSlot", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -1), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), Parent = bar })
    local function renderGroupIcon()
        iconSlot:ClearAllChildren()
        local iconSpec = (info.Icon and info.Icon ~= "") and info.Icon or Icons.FromTitle(info.Name, "main")
        local ic = Icons.Create(iconSlot, iconSpec, 20, nil, info.Name)
        ic.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        ic.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    end
    renderGroupIcon()
    Theme.OnRender(iconSlot, renderGroupIcon)
    local offset = 36
    local badgeWidth = 0
    if info.Badge then
        badgeWidth = #tostring(info.Badge) * 8 + 16
        local pill = Draw.Box("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0.5, -1), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(badgeWidth, 18), Parent = bar }, "Danger", "DangerDark", 4, 2)
        Draw.Text({ Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = pill }, "Body", 12, "OnAccent", tostring(info.Badge))
        badgeWidth += 6
    end
    local displayTitle = info.Name or ""
    if type(displayTitle) == "string" and utf8 and utf8.codes then
        local cutLen = 0
        local hasCut = false
        for _, cp in utf8.codes(displayTitle) do
            if (cp >= 0x2000 and not (cp >= 0x0E00 and cp <= 0x0E7F)) or cp == 0xFE0F or cp == 0x200D then
                cutLen = cutLen + #utf8.char(cp)
                hasCut = true
            elseif hasCut and cp == 0x20 then
                cutLen = cutLen + 1
            else
                break
            end
        end
        if cutLen > 0 then
            displayTitle = displayTitle:sub(cutLen + 1)
        end
    end
    Draw.Text({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(offset, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -(offset + 34 + badgeWidth), 1, -2), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = bar }, "Body", Util.TextSize("Group"), "Text", displayTitle)
    self.Chevron = Icons.Vector(bar, "chevron_down", 14, "Muted")
    self.Chevron.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
    self.Chevron.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0)
    self.Chevron.Rotation = collapsed and -90 or 0
    bar.Activated:Connect(function()
        self:SetCollapsed(not self.Collapsed)
    end)
end

function Groupbox:SetCollapsed(collapsed)
    collapsed = collapsed == true
    if self.Collapsed == collapsed then
        return
    end
    self.Collapsed = collapsed
    if collapsed then
        Popup.Close()
    end
    Anim.Tween(self.Open, { Value = collapsed and 0 or 1 }, Config.Tween.Collapse)
    Anim.Tween(self.Chevron, { Rotation = collapsed and -90 or 0 }, Config.Tween.Collapse)
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  PAYOMBOYZ FLOATING TOGGLE WIDGET (Cute 8-Bit Theme-Reactive Capsule)
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Float.State = {
    Visible = true,
    IconMode = "Logo", -- "Logo" or "Avatar"
    SubMode = "FPS",   -- "FPS" or "Time"
    StartTime = os.time(),
}

function Float.Clamp()
    local button = Float.Button
    if not button or not State.Gui then
        return
    end
    local viewport = State.Gui.AbsoluteSize
    if viewport.X <= 0 or viewport.Y <= 0 then
        return
    end
    local pos = button.Position
    local absX = viewport.X * pos.X.Scale + pos.X.Offset
    local absY = viewport.Y * pos.Y.Scale + pos.Y.Offset
    local width = button.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and button.AbsoluteSize.X or 195
    local height = button.AbsoluteSize.Y > 0 and button.AbsoluteSize.Y or 46
    local clampedX = math.clamp(absX, 8, math.max(8, viewport.X - width - 8))
    local clampedY = math.clamp(absY, 8, math.max(8, viewport.Y - height - 8))
    button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(clampedX + 0.5), math.floor(clampedY + 0.5))
end

function Float.Build()
    if Float.Button then
        pcall(function() Float.Button:Destroy() end)
    end

    local width, height = 195, 46
    local button = Draw.New("TextButton", {
        Name = "PayomboyZToggle",
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0.45, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height),
        ZIndex = Config.Layer.Float,
        Visible = Float.State.Visible,
        Parent = State.Gui,
    })

    -- 8-Bit Offset Shadow
    local shadow = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Name = "Shadow",
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 4),
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
        ZIndex = 1,
        Parent = button,
    }, "Shadow", nil, 12)

    -- Main Capsule Body
    local body = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Name = "Body",
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        ZIndex = 2,
        Parent = button,
    }, "BackdropAlt", "Outline", 12, 2)

    -- Top Gloss Highlight Stripe
    local gloss = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "Gloss",
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 2),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.75,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = 3,
        Parent = body,
    })
    Draw.Corner(gloss, 1)

    -- Content Row
    local content = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "Content",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 4),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -8),
        ZIndex = 4,
        Parent = body,
    })
    Draw.List(content, 8, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)

    -- 1. Mascot / Avatar Badge Slot
    local iconSlot = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Name = "IconSlot",
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34),
        LayoutOrder = 1,
        Parent = content,
    }, "Element", "Outline", UDim.new(1, 0), 1.5)

    local iconImg = Draw.New("ImageLabel", {
        Name = "MascotImage",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.88, 0.88),
        ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
        ZIndex = 5,
        Parent = iconSlot,
    })
    Draw.Corner(iconImg, UDim.new(1, 0))

    -- Mascot Cute Bobbing & Breathing Animation
    Anim.Loop("FloatMascot", iconImg, {
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.44),
        Rotation = 4,
    }, 1.2, "Sine", true)

    -- 2. Information Column (Title + SubInfo)
    local infoCol = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "InfoCol",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0),
        LayoutOrder = 2,
        Parent = content,
    })
    Draw.List(infoCol, 1, false, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)

    -- Top Row: PAYOMBOYZ Hub Name + Online Green Dot
    local topRow = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "TopRow",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
        LayoutOrder = 1,
        Parent = infoCol,
    })
    Draw.List(topRow, 5, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)

    local hubTitle = Draw.Text({
        Name = "HubTitle",
        Text = "PAYOMBOYZ",
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(88, 18),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        LayoutOrder = 1,
        Parent = topRow,
    }, "Logo", 13, "Accent")
    Draw.Stroke(hubTitle, "Ink", 1)

    local statusDot = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "StatusDot",
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 230, 120),
        LayoutOrder = 2,
        Parent = topRow,
    })
    Draw.Corner(statusDot, UDim.new(1, 0))
    Anim.Loop("FloatStatus", statusDot, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 }, 0.9, "Sine", true)

    -- Bottom Row: @Username + Telemetry Pill (FPS / Time)
    local bottomRow = Draw.New("Frame", {
        Name = "BottomRow",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
        LayoutOrder = 2,
        Parent = infoCol,
    })
    Draw.List(bottomRow, 4, true, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center)

    local lp = LocalPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
    local rawName = lp and ("@" .. (lp.Name or "PayomboyZ")) or "@PayomboyZ"
    local userLabel = Draw.Text({
        Name = "UserLabel",
        Text = rawName,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(68, 14),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        LayoutOrder = 1,
        Parent = bottomRow,
    }, "Desc", 10, "SubText")

    local sep = Draw.Text({
        Name = "Sep",
        Text = "•",
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 14),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        LayoutOrder = 2,
        Parent = bottomRow,
    }, "Desc", 9, "SubText")

    local teleBadge = Draw.Box("Frame", {
        Name = "TeleBadge",
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 14),
        LayoutOrder = 3,
        Parent = bottomRow,
    }, "Element", nil, 4)
    teleBadge.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4

    local teleLabel = Draw.Text({
        Name = "TeleLabel",
        Text = "60 FPS",
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        Parent = teleBadge,
    }, "Desc", 9, "Coin")

    Float.Button = button
    Float.Body = body
    Float.Shadow = shadow
    Float.IconImg = iconImg
    Float.IconSlot = iconSlot
    Float.HubTitle = hubTitle
    Float.UserLabel = userLabel
    Float.TeleLabel = teleLabel
    Float.Sprite = body

    -- Per-Theme Float Mascot Mapping (17+ Themes)
    local ThemeMascots = {
        ["Overworld"] = "mushroom",
        ["Castle"] = "knight_helm",
        ["Underground"] = "cavern_pick",
        ["Ice Land"] = "snowflake_crystal",
        ["Snow"] = "snowflake_crystal",
        ["Desert"] = "desert_sun",
        ["Ghost House"] = "boo",
        ["Star Road"] = "cosmic_star",
        ["Jungle"] = "bamboo_crate",
        ["Sky World"] = "angel_halo",
        ["Halloween"] = "pumpkin",
        ["Kirby"] = "kirby_star",
        ["Pokemon Cafe"] = "poke_ball",
        ["Animal Crossing"] = "ac_leaf",
        ["Stardew Valley"] = "sdv_stardrop",
        ["Tamagotchi"] = "tama_heart",
        ["Dark"] = "terminal",
        ["Light"] = "feather",
    }

    -- Render Icon Mode
    function Float.UpdateIcon()
        if not Float.IconSlot then return end
        if Float.State.IconMode == "Avatar" and lp and lp.UserId then
            if Float.MascotSprite then
                pcall(function() Float.MascotSprite:Destroy() end)
                Float.MascotSprite = nil
            end
            if not Float.IconImg then
                Float.IconImg = Draw.New("ImageLabel", {
                    Name = "MascotImage",
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                    Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
                    Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.88, 0.88),
                    ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                    ZIndex = 5,
                    Parent = Float.IconSlot,
                })
                Draw.Corner(Float.IconImg, UDim.new(1, 0))
            end
            Float.IconImg.Visible = true
            local ok, thumb = pcall(Players.GetUserThumbnailAsync, Players, lp.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
            if ok and thumb then
                Float.IconImg.Image = thumb
                return
            end
            Float.IconImg.Image = Assets.Resolve("logo_hug") or Assets.Resolve("float_logo") or "rbxassetid://87106942787227"
            return
        end

        -- Theme Mascot Sprite Mode
        if Float.IconImg then
            Float.IconImg.Visible = false
        end
        if Float.MascotSprite then
            pcall(function() Float.MascotSprite:Destroy() end)
            Float.MascotSprite = nil
        end
        local theme = State.ThemeName or "Overworld"
        local mascotKey = ThemeMascots[theme] or "coin"
        local sprite = Sprite.New(Float.IconSlot, mascotKey, 24)
        if sprite then
            sprite.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            sprite.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            sprite.ZIndex = 5
            Float.MascotSprite = sprite
        end
    end

    Float.UpdateIcon()

    -- Real-Time Telemetry Loop
    local frameCount = 0
    local lastCheck = tick()
    local fpsVal = 60
    local teleConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not Float.Button or not Float.Button.Parent then
            return
        end
        frameCount = frameCount + 1
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastCheck >= 0.5 then
            fpsVal = math.floor(frameCount / (now - lastCheck) + 0.5)
            frameCount = 0
            lastCheck = now
            if Float.TeleLabel and Float.TeleLabel.Parent then
                if Float.State.SubMode == "Time" then
                    local elapsed = os.time() - Float.State.StartTime
                    local m = math.floor(elapsed / 60)
                    local s = elapsed % 60
                    Float.TeleLabel.Text = string.format("%02d:%02d", m, s)
                else
                    Float.TeleLabel.Text = tostring(fpsVal) .. " FPS"
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    table.insert(State.Connections, teleConn)

    -- Theme Reactivity
    Theme.OnRender(button, function()
        if not Float.Body then return end
        Float.UpdateIcon()
        hubTitle.TextColor3 = Theme.Colors.Accent or Themes.Shared.White
        teleLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Colors.Coin or Themes.Shared.White
    end)

    -- Dragging & Clamping Logic
    local moved = false
    Gui.Draggable(button, function(begin, delta)
        if not delta then
            moved = false
            return button.Position
        end
        moved = moved or delta.Magnitude > Config.DragThreshold
        if moved then
            button.Position = begin + UDim2.fromOffset(delta.X, delta.Y)
            Float.Clamp()
        end
        return begin
    end)

    -- Click / Activation: Toggle Window
    button.Activated:Connect(function()
        if moved or not State.Window then
            return
        end
        Anim.Bump(Float.Body, 6)
        Anim.CoinPop(button, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0), 16)
        State.Window:Toggle()
    end)

    -- Hover feedback
    button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        Anim.Tween(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, -2) }, 0.14, "Out")
        Anim.Tween(iconImg, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.96, 0.96) }, 0.14, "Out")
    end)
    button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        Anim.Tween(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, 0.14, "Out")
        Anim.Tween(iconImg, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.88, 0.88) }, 0.14, "Out")
    end)
end

function Float.SetVisible(visible)
    Float.State.Visible = visible
    if Float.Button then
        Float.Button.Visible = visible
        if visible then
            Float.Clamp()
        end
    end
